     1	/**
     2	 * Child supervision — the loop's reliability surface. Owns the FULL child
     3	 * lifecycle: argv assembly (per-role permissions), detached process-group
     4	 * spawn with closed stdin, timeout SIGTERM→grace→SIGKILL of the whole group,
     5	 * capped transcript logs, region-bounded verdict parsing (prompt-echo-proof),
     6	 * and exit classification via core detect. Plan:
     7	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"Child supervision
     8	 * contract".
     9	 */
    10	import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
    11	import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
    12	import { dirname } from 'node:path';
    13	import { classify } from '../detect/classifier.mjs';
    14	
    15	// Every review seat is read-only — writers are the implementer-side roles
    16	// only. The merger CHILD is read-only too: it adversarially re-checks, but
    17	// the SUPERVISOR performs the actual merge via worktrees.mergeSubtask
    18	// (pipeline contract — the merger child never issues git).
    19	const REVIEWER_ROLES = new Set(['plan-reviewer', 'test-verifier', 'subtask-reviewer', 'final-reviewer-a', 'final-reviewer-b', 'merger']);
    20	
    21	// AGENTS.md §9: children commit solely as the repo owner, zero AI attribution.
    22	const GIT_IDENTITY = Object.freeze({
    23	  GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: 'SakshamUboweja',
    24	  GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: 'ssakshamu@gmail.com',
    25	  GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: 'SakshamUboweja',
    26	  GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: 'ssakshamu@gmail.com',
    27	});
    28	
    29	/**
    30	 * Assemble the spawn contract for a role child. Reviewers are read-only on
    31	 * every platform; every child is marked supervised (its own baton hooks
    32	 * no-op), carries the sole-author git identity, and has stdin CLOSED at
    33	 * spawn (the codex-exec stdin-hang field failure).
    34	 * @param {{platform: string, role: string, model: string, effort?: string | null, mode?: string}} assignment
    35	 * @param {string} prompt
    36	 * @param {{root: string}} opts
    37	 * @returns {{command: string, args: string[], env: Record<string, string>, stdio: any[]}}
    38	 */
    39	export function buildChildArgv(assignment, prompt, opts) {
    40	  const readOnly = REVIEWER_ROLES.has(assignment.role);
    41	  const env = { BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD: '1', ...GIT_IDENTITY };
    42	  const stdio = ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'];
    43	
    44	  if (assignment.platform === 'claude-code') {
    45	    const args = ['-p', prompt];
    46	    if (readOnly) {
    47	      args.push('--permission-mode', 'default', '--allowedTools', 'Read,Grep,Glob');
    48	    } else {
    49	      args.push('--permission-mode', 'acceptEdits', '--allowedTools', 'Read,Grep,Glob,Write,Edit,Bash');
    50	    }
    51	    return { command: 'claude', args, env, stdio };
    52	  }
    53	
    54	  // codex (and the default shape for exec-style CLIs).
    55	  const args = ['exec', '-C', opts.root, '-s', readOnly ? 'read-only' : 'workspace-write', '--model', assignment.model];
    56	  if (assignment.effort) args.push('-c', `model_reasoning_effort=${assignment.effort}`);
    57	  args.push(prompt);
    58	  return { command: 'codex', args, env, stdio };
    59	}
    60	
    61	const VERDICT_RE = /VERDICT:\s*(APPROVED_WITH_NOTES|APPROVED|BLOCKED)\b/g;
    62	
    63	/** @param {string} region @returns {{verdict: string, findings: string} | null} */
    64	function lastVerdictIn(region) {
    65	  let last = null;
    66	  for (const m of region.matchAll(VERDICT_RE)) last = m;
    67	  if (!last) return null;
    68	  const after = region.slice((last.index ?? 0) + last[0].length);
    69	  return { verdict: last[1], findings: after.trim() };
    70	}
    71	
    72	/**
    73	 * Region-bounded verdict parsing — the prompt region (which quotes the
    74	 * verdict format in its instructions) is NEVER scanned, and there is no
    75	 * whole-transcript fallback. Unparseable is BLOCKED, never APPROVED.
    76	 * - codex: only the text after the LAST line starting with 'tokens used'.
    77	 * - claude-code: only the `result` field of the structured JSON output.
    78	 * Multiple tails inside the trusted region → the LAST one wins.
    79	 * @param {string} transcript @param {{platform: string}} opts
    80	 * @returns {{verdict: string, findings: string, reason?: string}}
    81	 */
    82	export function parseVerdict(transcript, { platform }) {
    83	  const unparseable = { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: '', reason: 'unparseable' };
    84	  const text = String(transcript ?? '');
    85	
    86	  if (platform === 'claude-code') {
    87	    let obj = null;
    88	    try {
    89	      obj = JSON.parse(text.trim());
    90	    } catch {
    91	      return unparseable;
    92	    }
    93	    if (typeof obj?.result !== 'string') return unparseable;
    94	    const hit = lastVerdictIn(obj.result);
    95	    return hit ?? unparseable;
    96	  }
    97	
    98	  // codex: locate the LAST 'tokens used' marker line; the trusted region is
    99	  // strictly after it.
   100	  const lines = text.split('\n');
   101	  let markerLine = -1;
   102	  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
   103	    if (/^tokens used\b/i.test(lines[i])) markerLine = i;
   104	  }
   105	  if (markerLine === -1) return unparseable;
   106	  const region = lines.slice(markerLine + 1).join('\n');
   107	  const hit = lastVerdictIn(region);
   108	  return hit ?? unparseable;
   109	}
   110	
   111	const TRUNCATION_MARKER = '\n[... truncated by baton log cap ...]\n';
   112	
   113	/**
   114	 * Cap a transcript: head preserved, middle replaced with a marker, TAIL
   115	 * preserved verbatim (the verdict lives at the end and must never be cut).
   116	 * @param {string} text @param {number} maxBytes
   117	 * @returns {string}
   118	 */
   119	export function capLog(text, maxBytes) {
   120	  const s = String(text ?? '');
   121	  if (Buffer.byteLength(s) <= maxBytes) return s;
   122	  const headBytes = Math.max(1, Math.floor(maxBytes * 0.4));
   123	  const tailBytes = Math.max(1, maxBytes - headBytes);
   124	  const buf = Buffer.from(s);
   125	  const head = buf.subarray(0, headBytes).toString();
   126	  const tail = buf.subarray(buf.length - tailBytes).toString();
   127	  return head + TRUNCATION_MARKER + tail;
   128	}
   129	
   130	/**
   131	 * Exit classification delegates to core detect — one classifier, one
   132	 * signature table, no drift. Returns exactly classify(...).class.
   133	 * @param {string} transcript @param {number} exitCode @param {string} platform @param {any} table
   134	 * @returns {string}
   135	 */
   136	export function classifyChildExit(transcript, exitCode, platform, table) {
   137	  return classify({ text: transcript, exitCode, platform, table }).class;
   138	}
   139	
   140	/** @param {number} pid @param {NodeJS.Signals} sig */
   141	function killGroup(pid, sig) {
   142	  try {
   143	    process.kill(-pid, sig);
   144	  } catch {
   145	    // ESRCH — already gone.
   146	  }
   147	}
   148	
   149	/**
   150	 * Spawn and supervise one child: detached in its OWN process group, stdin
   151	 * closed; on timeout SIGTERM the group, wait graceMs, SIGKILL the group (no
   152	 * zombie grandchildren). Output streams to a capped log at opts.logPath; the
   153	 * verdict is parsed from the full transcript, region-bounded.
   154	 * @param {{command: string, args: string[], env?: Record<string, string>, cwd?: string}} spec
   155	 * @param {{timeoutMs: number, graceMs: number, logPath: string, maxLogBytes?: number, platform?: string}} opts
   156	 * @returns {Promise<{timedOut: boolean, exitCode: number | null, verdict?: string, findings?: string, reason?: string, logPath: string}>}
   157	 */
   158	export function superviseChild(spec, opts) {
   159	  return new Promise((resolve) => {
   160	    const maxLogBytes = opts.maxLogBytes ?? 1_000_000;
   161	    const child = spawn(spec.command, spec.args, {
   162	      cwd: spec.cwd,
   163	      env: { ...process.env, ...(spec.env ?? {}) },
   164	      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
   165	      detached: true, // its own process group — the kill unit
   166	    });
   167	
   168	    let out = '';
   169	    let timedOut = false;
   170	    /** @type {NodeJS.Timeout | null} */
   171	    let killTimer = null;
   172	
   173	    const onChunk = (/** @type {Buffer} */ c) => {
   174	      out += c.toString();
   175	    };
   176	    child.stdout?.on('data', onChunk);
   177	    child.stderr?.on('data', onChunk);
   178	
   179	    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
   180	      timedOut = true;
   181	      if (typeof child.pid === 'number') {
   182	        killGroup(child.pid, 'SIGTERM');
   183	        killTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   184	          if (typeof child.pid === 'number') killGroup(child.pid, 'SIGKILL');
   185	        }, opts.graceMs);
   186	      }
   187	    }, opts.timeoutMs);
   188	
   189	    const finish = (/** @type {number | null} */ exitCode) => {
   190	      clearTimeout(timeout);
   191	      if (killTimer) clearTimeout(killTimer);
   192	      // Belt-and-braces: reap any group survivors even on a clean exit path.
   193	      if (timedOut && typeof child.pid === 'number') killGroup(child.pid, 'SIGKILL');
   194	      try {
   195	        mkdirSync(dirname(opts.logPath), { recursive: true });
   196	        writeFileSync(opts.logPath, capLog(out, maxLogBytes));
   197	      } catch {
   198	        // The log must never mask the result.
   199	      }
   200	      const parsed = parseVerdict(out, { platform: opts.platform ?? 'codex' });
   201	      resolve({
   202	        timedOut,
   203	        exitCode: timedOut ? null : exitCode,
   204	        verdict: parsed.verdict,
   205	        findings: parsed.findings,
   206	        ...(parsed.reason ? { reason: parsed.reason } : {}),
   207	        logPath: opts.logPath,
   208	      });
   209	    };
   210	
   211	    child.on('error', () => finish(null));
   212	    child.on('close', (code) => finish(code));
   213	  });
   214	}

     1	/**
     2	 * `baton pipeline run` — the Layer-3 dual-worktree preset over the loop
     3	 * engine. Subtasks alternate writer seats (index 0 → wt-a/worker-a, 1 →

    27	const STRING_FLAGS = new Set(['root']);
    28	const EXIT_ESCALATED = 3;
    29	const EXIT_PARKED = 4;

    31	/**
    32	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    33	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    34	 */
    35	export async function cmdPipeline(args, io) {
    36	  /** @type {string[]} */
    37	  const positionals = [];
    38	  /** @type {string[]} */
    39	  const flagTokens = [];
    40	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
    41	    const a = args[i];
    42	    if (typeof a === 'string' && a.startsWith('--')) {
    43	      flagTokens.push(a);
    44	      if (STRING_FLAGS.has(a.slice(2)) && i + 1 < args.length) {
    45	        i += 1;
    46	        flagTokens.push(args[i]);
    47	      }
    48	    } else {
    49	      positionals.push(a);
    50	    }
    51	  }
    52	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(flagTokens, {});
    53	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'pipeline', parsed.error);
    54	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    55	
    56	  const sub = positionals[0];
    57	  if (sub === undefined) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', 'a subcommand is required — try: baton pipeline run');
    58	  if (sub !== 'run') return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `unknown subcommand '${sub}' (supported: run)`);
    59	  if (positionals.length > 1) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `unexpected argument '${positionals[1]}'`);
    60	
    61	  return runPipeline(flags, io);
    62	}
    63	
    64	/** Validate the spec's subtasks — every rejection names its offender.
    65	 * @param {any} subtasks @returns {string | null} */
    66	function validateSubtasks(subtasks) {
    67	  if (!Array.isArray(subtasks) || subtasks.length === 0) {
    68	    return 'loop.json needs a non-empty `subtasks` array ([{id, title}]) for a pipeline run';
    69	  }
    70	  const seen = new Set();
    71	  for (let i = 0; i < subtasks.length; i += 1) {
    72	    const st = subtasks[i];
    73	    if (typeof st?.id !== 'string' || st.id.length === 0) return `subtasks[${i}] is missing a string id`;
    74	    if (typeof st?.title !== 'string' || st.title.length === 0) return `subtasks[${i}] ('${st.id}') is missing a string title`;
    75	    if (seen.has(st.id)) return `subtasks carry a duplicate id '${st.id}' — ids name branches and must be unique`;
    76	    seen.add(st.id);
    77	  }
    78	  return null;
    79	}

    81	/**
    82	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags @param {any} io
    83	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    84	 */
    85	async function runPipeline(flags, io) {
    86	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
    87	  const p = loopPaths(root);
    88	
    89	  const rawSpec = safeReadJson(io.fs, `${root}/loop.json`);
    90	  if (!rawSpec.ok) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `no loop.json at ${root} — scaffold one with: baton loop init "<goal>"`);
    91	  const spec = rawSpec.value;
    92	  const subtaskError = validateSubtasks(spec?.subtasks);
    93	  if (subtaskError !== null) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', subtaskError);
    94	  const { config } = loadConfig(root, io);
    95	  if (!config) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `no readable baton.config.json at ${root} — the role matrix is required`);
    96	  const cap = typeof spec?.budgets?.iterationCap === 'number' ? spec.budgets.iterationCap : 5;
    97	  const timeoutMs = (typeof spec?.budgets?.perRoleTimeoutMin === 'number' ? spec.budgets.perRoleTimeoutMin : 30) * 60_000;
    98	
    99	  /** @param {string} role @returns {any | null} */
   100	  const resolveOne = (role) => {
   101	    const a = resolveRoles({ config, to: 'claude-code', avoid: [], avoidEntries: [], probes: null }).assignments[role];
   102	    return a && a.mode !== 'unavailable' ? a : null;
   103	  };
   104	
   105	  // The synthesized phase list makes the loop state machine (phase count,
   106	  // done detection, journal replay) work unchanged for the subtask cycle.
   107	  const subtasks = spec.subtasks;
   108	  const stateSpec = {
   109	    goal: spec.goal,
   110	    phases: subtasks.map((/** @type {any} */ st, /** @type {number} */ i) => ({ id: `subtask-${st.id}`, role: `worker-${i % 2 === 0 ? 'a' : 'b'}` })),
   111	  };
   112	  /** @type {any} */
   113	  let state = initLoopState(stateSpec, io);
   114	  await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
   115	  const runner = io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild;
   116	  const seats = worktreePaths(root).seats;
   117	  let childSeq = 0;
   118	
   119	  const transition = async (/** @type {any} */ ev) => {
   120	    const next = applyLoopEvent(state, ev);
   121	    const seq = await appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io);
   122	    state = { ...next, journalSeq: seq };
   123	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
   124	    return state;
   125	  };
   126	  const park = async (/** @type {string} */ reason) => {
   127	    await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason });
   128	    io.stderr.write(`baton pipeline run: parked — ${reason}\n`);
   129	    return EXIT_PARKED;
   130	  };
   131	
   132	  await setupWorktrees(root, io);
   133	
   134	  const spawn = async (/** @type {any} */ assignment, /** @type {string} */ prompt, /** @type {string} */ seatPath, /** @type {string} */ label) => {
   135	    childSeq += 1;
   136	    const logPath = `${p.dir}/children/${String(childSeq).padStart(3, '0')}-${label}.log`;
   137	    ensureDir(io.fs, `${p.dir}/children`);
   138	    const childSpec = buildChildArgv(assignment, prompt, { root: seatPath });
   139	    return runner(childSpec, { timeoutMs, graceMs: 10_000, logPath, maxLogBytes: 1_000_000, platform: assignment.platform });
   140	  };
   141	
   142	  for (let i = 0; i < subtasks.length; i += 1) {
   143	    const st = subtasks[i];
   144	    const seat = i % 2 === 0 ? 'a' : 'b';
   145	    const other = seat === 'a' ? 'b' : 'a';
   146	    const branch = `baton/wt-${seat}/subtask-${st.id}`;
   147	    const gate = `subtask-${st.id}-review`;
   148	
   149	    const writerAssignment = resolveOne(`worker-${seat}`);
   150	    const reviewerModel = resolveOne(`worker-${other}`);
   151	    const mergerModel = resolveOne('merger');
   152	    if (!writerAssignment || !reviewerModel || !mergerModel) {
   153	      return park(`no eligible assignment for subtask '${st.id}' (worker-${seat}/worker-${other}/merger must all resolve)`);
   154	    }
   155	
   156	    // Start the subtask branch in the writer's seat, then verify the seat.
   157	    await io.execFile('git', ['checkout', '-b', branch], { cwd: seats[seat] });
   158	    const pre = await preflightWorktree(root, { seat, branch }, io);
   159	    if (pre.ok !== true) return park(`preflight refused subtask '${st.id}': ${pre.refusal}`);
   160	    const mainSha = (await io.execFile('git', ['rev-parse', 'main'], { cwd: root })).stdout.trim();
   161	
   162	    let findings = '';
   163	    let merged = false;
   164	    while (!merged) {
   165	      if ((state.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0) >= cap) {
   166	        await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   167	        atomicWriteText(
   168	          io.fs,
   169	          `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`,
   170	          `# Pipeline escalation\n\nGate '${gate}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`,
   171	        );
   172	        io.stderr.write(`baton pipeline run: gate '${gate}' hit the ${cap}-iteration cap — escalated (see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md)\n`);
   173	        return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   174	      }
   175	
   176	      // Writer (write-capable, its own seat).
   177	      const writerPrompt = [
   178	        `You are the writer (worker-${seat}) for subtask '${st.id}': ${st.title}.`,
   179	        `Goal: ${spec.goal}. Work ONLY on branch ${branch} in your worktree; commit your work there.`,
   180	        findings ? `The previous attempt was BLOCKED — address every finding:\n${findings}` : '',
   181	      ]
   182	        .filter(Boolean)
   183	        .join('\n\n');
   184	      await spawn({ ...writerAssignment, role: `worker-${seat}` }, writerPrompt, seats[seat], `${st.id}-writer`);
   185	
   186	      const post = await postflightWorktree(root, { seat, branch, mainSha }, io);
   187	      if (post.ok !== true) return park(`postflight refused subtask '${st.id}': ${post.refusal}`);
   188	
   189	      // Reviewer (read-only, the OTHER seat's model and cwd, subtask-reviewer role).
   190	      const reviewPrompt = [
   191	        `You are the subtask-reviewer for subtask '${st.id}': ${st.title}.`,
   192	        `Review the diff of ${branch} against main from the ${other} seat (fresh context).`,
   193	        "End with exactly:\nVERDICT: APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED\nFINDINGS: numbered findings, or 'none'",
   194	      ].join('\n\n');
   195	      const review = await spawn({ ...reviewerModel, role: 'subtask-reviewer' }, reviewPrompt, seats[other], `${st.id}-review`);
   196	      if (review.verdict !== 'APPROVED' && review.verdict !== 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
   197	        findings = String(review.findings ?? '');
   198	        const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   199	        if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
   200	          atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`, `# Pipeline escalation\n\nGate '${gate}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`);
   201	          return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   202	        }
   203	        continue;
   204	      }
   205	
   206	      // Merger (read-only child — it re-checks; the SUPERVISOR merges).
   207	      const mergerPrompt = [
   208	        `You are the merger for subtask '${st.id}'. Adversarially re-check ${branch} against main before it merges.`,
   209	        "End with exactly:\nVERDICT: APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED\nFINDINGS: numbered findings, or 'none'",
   210	      ].join('\n\n');
   211	      const mergerVerdict = await spawn({ ...mergerModel, role: 'merger' }, mergerPrompt, root, `${st.id}-merge-check`);
   212	      if (mergerVerdict.verdict !== 'APPROVED' && mergerVerdict.verdict !== 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
   213	        findings = String(mergerVerdict.findings ?? '');
   214	        const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   215	        if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
   216	          atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`, `# Pipeline escalation\n\nGate '${gate}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`);
   217	          return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   218	        }
   219	        continue;
   220	      }
   221	
   222	      const merge = await mergeSubtask(root, { branch }, io);
   223	      if (merge.ok !== true) return park(`merge of '${branch}' refused: ${merge.reason}`);
   224	      merged = true;
   225	    }
   226	
   227	    await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE });
   228	  }
   229	
   230	  if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { status: state.status, subtasks: subtasks.length } });
   231	  else io.stdout.write(`baton pipeline run: done — ${subtasks.length} subtask(s) merged\n`);
   232	  return state.status === LOOP_STATUS.DONE ? 0 : state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED ? EXIT_PARKED : 0;
   233	}

    29	// Flags that consume a value — needed to split positionals from flag tokens
    30	// without mistaking a flag's value for a positional. Keep in sync with the
    31	// strict spec below plus the implied common string flag (--root).
    32	const STRING_FLAGS = new Set(['root', 'approve-smoke']);
    33	
    34	/**
    35	 * `baton loop <subcommand>` — the Layer-2 goal-loop surface. v1 subcommands:
    36	 * `init "<goal>"` (scaffold loop.json; two-phase, idempotent, dry-run-able).
    37	 * Supervisor-side by design: the BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD guard deliberately
    38	 * does NOT apply here.
    39	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    40	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    41	 */
    42	export async function cmdLoop(args, io) {
    43	  /** @type {string[]} */
    44	  const positionals = [];
    45	  /** @type {string[]} */
    46	  const flagTokens = [];
    47	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
    48	    const a = args[i];
    49	    if (typeof a === 'string' && a.startsWith('--')) {
    50	      flagTokens.push(a);
    51	      if (STRING_FLAGS.has(a.slice(2)) && i + 1 < args.length) {
    52	        i += 1;
    53	        flagTokens.push(args[i]);
    54	      }
    55	    } else {
    56	      positionals.push(a);
    57	    }
    58	  }
    59	
    60	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(flagTokens, { 'dry-run': 'boolean', 'approve-smoke': 'string' });
    61	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'loop', parsed.error);
    62	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    63	
    64	  const sub = positionals[0];
    65	  if (sub === undefined) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'a subcommand is required — try: baton loop init "<goal>" or baton loop run');
    66	  if (sub === 'run') {
    67	    if (positionals.length > 1) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `unexpected argument '${positionals[1]}'`);
    68	    if (flags['approve-smoke'] === '') return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', '--approve-smoke requires a value (the token from smoke-approval.json)');
    69	    return runLoop(flags, io);
    70	  }
    71	  if (sub !== 'init') return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `unknown subcommand '${sub}' (supported: init, run)`);
    72	
    73	  const goal = positionals[1];
    74	  if (goal === undefined) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'loop init requires a goal — baton loop init "<goal>"');
    75	  if (positionals.length > 2) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `unexpected argument '${positionals[2]}'`);
    76	
    77	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
    78	  const path = `${root}/loop.json`;
    79	
    80	  const finish = (/** @type {any} */ data, /** @type {string} */ human) => {
    81	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data });
    82	    else io.stdout.write(`${human}\n`);
    83	    return 0;
    84	  };
    85	
    86	  // Idempotent: an existing spec is the user's — never overwritten.
    87	  if (io.fs.existsSync(path)) {
    88	    return finish({ created: false, path }, `baton loop init: ${path} already exists — left untouched`);
    89	  }
    90	  if (flags['dry-run'] === true) {
    91	    return finish(
    92	      { created: false, path, dryRun: true },
    93	      `baton loop init (dry-run): would write ${path} — the default baton/loop@1 spec with goal "${goal}"`,
    94	    );
    95	  }
    96	  ensureDir(io.fs, root);
    97	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, path, defaultLoopSpec(goal));
    98	  return finish({ created: true, path }, `baton loop init: wrote ${path} (goal "${goal}") — edit constraints/smoke, then run baton loop run`);
    99	}
   100	
   101	// Exit codes for `loop run` (frozen by the loop-run test contract):
   102	// 0 done / awaiting-smoke-approval · 1 live-lock refusal · 2 usage error ·
   103	// 3 escalated · 4 parked.
   104	const EXIT_ESCALATED = 3;
   105	const EXIT_PARKED = 4;
   106	
   107	/** @param {any} g */
   108	const gitHeadOf = (g) => g?.headSha ?? 'none';
   109	/** @param {any} g */
   110	const gitContentOf = (g) => g?.contentDigest ?? dedupeKey(g ?? null);
   111	
   112	/**
   113	 * The supervisor state machine: drives the spec's phases through headless
   114	 * role children, records every transition in the journal-replayable loop
   115	 * state, enforces the 5-cap, pauses at the smoke gate, and hands limit
   116	 * deaths to the failover transaction. Children run via the injectable seam
   117	 * `io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild`.
   118	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags @param {any} io
   119	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
   120	 */
   121	async function runLoop(flags, io) {
   122	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
   123	  const specPath = `${root}/loop.json`;
   124	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   125	  const lockPath = `${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`;
   126	
   127	  // Preconditions — before any lock or spawn.
   128	  const rawSpec = safeReadJson(io.fs, specPath);
   129	  if (!rawSpec.ok) {
   130	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `no loop.json at ${specPath} — scaffold one with: baton loop init "<goal>"`);
   131	  }
   132	  const { config } = loadConfig(root, io);
   133	  if (!config) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `no readable baton.config.json at ${root} — the role matrix is required`);
   134	  const validated = validateLoopSpec(rawSpec.value, config);
   135	  if (validated.ok !== true) {
   136	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `loop.json is invalid:\n  - ${validated.errors.join('\n  - ')}`);
   137	  }
   138	  const spec = validated.spec;
   139	
   140	  // Supervisor run lock — same provably-dead discipline as the bundle lock:
   141	  // a live same-host owner is refused; a dead one is reclaimed.
   142	  const existingLock = safeReadJson(io.fs, lockPath);
   143	  if (existingLock.ok && existingLock.value && typeof existingLock.value.pid === 'number') {
   144	    const other = existingLock.value;
   145	    const alive = typeof io.processAlive === 'function' ? io.processAlive(other.pid) : true;
   146	    const sameProcess = other.pid === io.pid && other.startTime === io.startTime;
   147	    if (!sameProcess && (other.host !== io.host || alive)) {
   148	      io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: another supervisor (pid ${other.pid} on ${other.host}, run ${other.runId}) holds the run lock — refusing a second supervisor\n`);
   149	      return 1;
   150	    }
   151	    io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: reclaimed the run lock from a provably-dead supervisor (pid ${other.pid})\n`);
   152	  }
   153	
   154	  // Load or initialize the run state.
   155	  let { state } = await loadLoopState(root, io);
   156	  if (state === null) {
   157	    state = initLoopState(spec, io);
   158	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
   159	  }
   160	  const runId = state.runId;
   161	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   162	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, lockPath, { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, runId });
   163	
   164	  /** Apply one event, journal it, persist the snapshot — one transition. */
   165	  const transition = async (/** @type {any} */ ev) => {
   166	    const next = applyLoopEvent(state, ev);
   167	    const seq = await appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io);
   168	    state = { ...next, journalSeq: seq };
   169	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
   170	    return state;
   171	  };
   172	
   173	  const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
   174	  const runner = io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild;
   175	  const sessionHint = `loop-${runId}`;
   176	  /** @type {string[]} */
   177	  let avoid = [];
   178	  /** @type {Array<{platform: string, model: string}>} */
   179	  let avoidEntries = [];
   180	  let childSeq = 0;
   181	
   182	  try {
   183	    // --approve-smoke: verify the presented token against RECOMPUTED inputs;
   184	    // drift parks (the human approved a state that no longer exists).
   185	    if (typeof flags['approve-smoke'] === 'string') {
   186	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) {
   187	        return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `--approve-smoke only applies while the run awaits smoke approval (status: ${state.status})`);
   188	      }
   189	      const record = safeReadJson(io.fs, `${p.dir}/smoke-approval.json`);
   190	      if (!record.ok) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'no smoke-approval.json found — run the loop to the smoke gate first');
   191	      const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   192	      const currentInputs = {
   193	        stateDigest: dedupeKey(io.fs.readFileSync(p.state, 'utf8')),
   194	        smokeCmd: String(spec.smoke?.cmd ?? ''),
   195	        smokeOutputDigest: String(record.value?.inputs?.smokeOutputDigest ?? ''),
   196	        gitHead: gitHeadOf(git),
   197	        gitContentDigest: gitContentOf(git),
   198	        childAssignment: String(record.value?.inputs?.childAssignment ?? ''),
   199	      };
   200	      const verify = verifySmokeToken(String(flags['approve-smoke']), currentInputs);
   201	      if (verify.ok !== true) {
   202	        const drifted = 'driftedInputs' in verify ? verify.driftedInputs.join(', ') : 'unknown';
   203	        await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: `smoke approval token is stale — drifted inputs: ${drifted}; re-run the smoke gate` });
   204	        io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: smoke approval REFUSED — inputs drifted since the token was issued (${drifted}); the run is parked for a fresh smoke pass\n`);
   205	        return EXIT_PARKED;
   206	      }
   207	      await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE, token: flags['approve-smoke'], verified: true });
   208	      io.stdout.write('baton loop run: smoke approval verified — continuing the run\n');
   209	    }
   210	
   211	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
   212	      io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: the run is escalated (gate ${state.escalation?.gate}) — see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md\n`);
   213	      return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   214	    }
   215	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) {
   216	      io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: the run is parked — ${state.parkReason ?? 'no reason recorded'}\n`);
   217	      return EXIT_PARKED;
   218	    }
   219	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) {
   220	      io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: awaiting smoke approval — resume with: baton loop run --approve-smoke <token from ${p.dir}/smoke-approval.json>\n`);
   221	      return 0;
   222	    }
   223	
   224	    // ---- The phase loop. ---------------------------------------------------
   225	    while (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING && state.phaseIndex < state.phaseCount) {
   226	      const phase = spec.phases[state.phaseIndex];
   227	      const cap = spec.budgets.iterationCap;
   228	      let findings = '';
   229	      let failoverAttempt = 0;
   230	      /** @type {any | null} */
   231	      let forcedAssignment = null;
   232	      /** @type {string | null} */
   233	      let forcedPrompt = null;
   234	      let phaseDone = false;
   235	
   236	      while (!phaseDone) {
   237	        // The cap gates the SPAWN: at the cap the reducer escalates and a
   238	        // 6th child never starts.
   239	        if ((state.iterations?.[phase.id] ?? 0) >= cap) {
   240	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: phase.id, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   241	          atomicWriteText(
   242	            io.fs,
   243	            `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`,
   244	            `# Loop escalation\n\nGate '${phase.id}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap on run ${runId}.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n\nResolve the findings, then resume with baton loop run.\n`,
   245	          );
   246	          io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: gate '${phase.id}' hit the ${cap}-iteration cap — escalated (see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md)\n`);
   247	          return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   248	        }
   249	        if (childSeq >= spec.budgets.maxChildrenPerPhase * spec.phases.length) {
   250	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: 'total child budget exhausted' });
   251	          return EXIT_PARKED;
   252	        }
   253	
   254	        const resolved =
   255	          forcedAssignment ??
   256	          (() => {
   257	            const r = resolveRoles({ config, to: 'claude-code', avoid, avoidEntries, probes: null }).assignments[phase.role];
   258	            return r && r.mode !== 'unavailable' ? r : null;
   259	          })();
   260	        if (!resolved) {
   261	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: `no eligible platform/model for role '${phase.role}'` });
   262	          io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: no eligible assignment for role '${phase.role}' — parked\n`);
   263	          return EXIT_PARKED;
   264	        }
   265	        const assignment = { ...resolved, role: phase.role };
   266	        const prompt =
   267	          forcedPrompt ??
   268	          [
   269	            `You are the ${phase.role} for loop run ${runId}, phase '${phase.id}'.`,
   270	            `Goal: ${spec.goal}`,
   271	            spec.constraints.length > 0 ? `Constraints:\n${spec.constraints.map((/** @type {string} */ c) => `- ${c}`).join('\n')}` : '',
   272	            findings ? `The previous attempt was BLOCKED with these findings — address every one:\n${findings}` : '',
   273	            "End with exactly:\nVERDICT: APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED\nFINDINGS: numbered findings, or 'none'",
   274	          ]
   275	            .filter(Boolean)
   276	            .join('\n\n');
   277	        forcedAssignment = null;
   278	        forcedPrompt = null;
   279	
   280	        childSeq += 1;
   281	        const logPath = `${p.dir}/children/${String(childSeq).padStart(3, '0')}-${phase.id}.log`;
   282	        ensureDir(io.fs, `${p.dir}/children`);
   283	        const childSpec = buildChildArgv(assignment, prompt, { root });
   284	        const result = await runner(childSpec, {
   285	          timeoutMs: spec.budgets.perRoleTimeoutMin * 60_000,
   286	          graceMs: 10_000,
   287	          logPath,
   288	          maxLogBytes: 1_000_000,
   289	          platform: assignment.platform,
   290	        });
   291	
   292	        // Classification reads the child's LOG (the frozen transcript), so a
   293	        // limit banner routes to failover even when a verdict parsed.
   294	        const transcript = io.fs.existsSync(logPath) ? io.fs.readFileSync(logPath, 'utf8') : '';
   295	        const cls = classify({ text: transcript, exitCode: result.exitCode ?? 0, platform: assignment.platform, table }).class;
   296	
   297	        if (cls === 'usage-limit' || cls === 'model-unavailable' || cls === 'other-error' || cls === 'throttle' || cls === 'auth') {
   298	          failoverAttempt += 1;
   299	          const decision = await runFailover({
   300	            root,
   301	            io,
   302	            config,
   303	            role: phase.role,
   304	            assignment,
   305	            transcript,
   306	            exitCode: result.exitCode ?? 1,
   307	            sessionHint,
   308	            probes: null,
   309	            avoid,
   310	            avoidEntries,
   311	            attempt: failoverAttempt,
   312	            loopState: { runId, phaseIndex: state.phaseIndex, iterations: state.iterations, status: state.status },
   313	          });
   314	          if (decision.action === 'park') {
   315	            await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: decision.reason });
   316	            io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: parked — ${decision.reason}${'resumeAt' in decision && decision.resumeAt ? ` (resume around ${decision.resumeAt})` : ''}\n`);
   317	            return EXIT_PARKED;
   318	          }
   319	          if (decision.action === 'relaunch') {
   320	            // Platform avoidance is PER-DEATH, not cumulative: runFailover
   321	            // already avoided the just-died platform when it resolved the
   322	            // relaunch. Accumulating here would exhaust every platform on a
   323	            // chained A→B→A failover and park instead of returning to A
   324	            // (acceptance constraint 2). Entry-level avoidance (dead MODELS)
   325	            // does accumulate — a rejected model stays rejected.
   326	            if (Array.isArray(decision.avoidEntries)) avoidEntries = decision.avoidEntries;
   327	            forcedAssignment = { ...decision.assignment, role: phase.role };
   328	            forcedPrompt = typeof decision.prompt === 'string' && decision.prompt.length > 0 ? decision.prompt : null;
   329	            continue; // relaunch the same phase on the new assignment
   330	          }
   331	          continue; // retry: re-spawn the same phase once more
   332	        }
   333	
   334	        const verdict = String(result.verdict ?? 'BLOCKED');
   335	        if (verdict === 'APPROVED' || verdict === 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
   336	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE });
   337	          phaseDone = true;
   338	
   339	          // The smoke gate fires right after the smoke-build phase completes.
   340	          if (phase.id === 'smoke-build' && typeof spec.smoke?.cmd === 'string' && spec.smoke.cmd.length > 0) {
   341	            const [cmd, ...cmdArgs] = spec.smoke.cmd.split(/\s+/);
   342	            let smokeOut = '';
   343	            try {
   344	              const r = await io.execFile(cmd, cmdArgs, { cwd: root });
   345	              smokeOut = `${r?.stdout ?? ''}${r?.stderr ?? ''}`;
   346	            } catch (err) {
   347	              smokeOut = `smoke command failed: ${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err)}`;
   348	            }
   349	            await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT });
   350	            const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   351	            const inputs = {
   352	              stateDigest: dedupeKey(io.fs.readFileSync(p.state, 'utf8')),
   353	              smokeCmd: spec.smoke.cmd,
   354	              smokeOutputDigest: dedupeKey(smokeOut),
   355	              gitHead: gitHeadOf(git),
   356	              gitContentDigest: gitContentOf(git),
   357	              childAssignment: JSON.stringify({ platform: assignment.platform, model: assignment.model }),
   358	            };
   359	            const token = smokeApprovalToken(inputs);
   360	            atomicWriteJson(io.fs, `${p.dir}/smoke-approval.json`, { token, inputs, issuedAt: io.now() });
   361	            atomicWriteText(
   362	              io.fs,
   363	              `${p.dir}/SMOKE-REVIEW.md`,
   364	              `# Smoke gate — human approval required\n\nRun: ${runId}\nCommand: \`${spec.smoke.cmd}\`\nExpected: ${spec.smoke.expect ?? '(unspecified)'}\n\n## Output\n\n\`\`\`\n${smokeOut}\n\`\`\`\n\nApprove with:\n\n    baton loop run --approve-smoke ${token}\n\nThe token is bound to the current state/output/git — any drift refuses it.\n`,
   365	            );
   366	            io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: smoke gate reached — review ${p.dir}/SMOKE-REVIEW.md and approve with --approve-smoke <token>\n`);
   367	            return 0;
   368	          }
   369	        } else {
   370	          // BLOCKED (or unparseable, already coerced to BLOCKED upstream).
   371	          findings = String(result.findings ?? '');
   372	          const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: phase.id, verdict });
   373	          if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
   374	            atomicWriteText(
   375	              io.fs,
   376	              `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`,
   377	              `# Loop escalation\n\nGate '${phase.id}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap on run ${runId}.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`,
   378	            );
   379	            io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: gate '${phase.id}' escalated at the cap — see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md\n`);
   380	            return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   381	          }
   382	        }
   383	      }
   384	    }
   385	
   386	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.DONE || state.phaseIndex >= state.phaseCount) {
   387	      io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: done — every phase completed (run ${runId})\n`);
   388	      return 0;
   389	    }
   390	    io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: stopped in status ${state.status}\n`);
   391	    return state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED ? EXIT_PARKED : state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED ? EXIT_ESCALATED : 0;
   392	  } finally {
   393	    // The lock is per-invocation: release on every exit path so a paused run
   394	    // (awaiting approval) can be resumed by the next invocation.
   395	    try {
   396	      if (io.fs.existsSync(lockPath)) io.fs.unlinkSync(lockPath);
   397	    } catch {
   398	      // Best effort — a stale lock is recoverable via the provably-dead path.
   399	    }
   400	  }
   401	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Loop run state — snapshot + journal + replay for the Layer-2 supervisor,
     3	 * mirroring the bundle store's crash-tolerance idioms (atomic tmp+rename
     4	 * snapshots, torn-tail-tolerant NDJSON, seq-dedup replay). Everything lives
     5	 * under <root>/.handoff/loop/; the supervisor is the sole writer. Plan:
     6	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"baton loop",
     7	 * §"Smoke gate", §"Supervisor lifetime and recovery", §"Caps".
     8	 */
     9	import { appendEntry, readAllTolerant } from '../util/jsonl.mjs';
    10	import { atomicWriteJson, safeReadJson, ensureDir } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
    11	import { dedupeKey } from '../util/ids.mjs';
    12	
    13	export const LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA = 'baton/loop-state@1';
    14	
    15	/** The frozen status vocabulary. */
    16	export const LOOP_STATUS = Object.freeze({
    17	  RUNNING: 'running',
    18	  AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL: 'awaiting-smoke-approval',
    19	  PARKED: 'parked',
    20	  ESCALATED: 'escalated',
    21	  DONE: 'done',
    22	});
    23	
    24	/** The frozen journal event vocabulary. */
    25	export const LOOP_EVENT = Object.freeze({
    26	  PHASE_ADVANCE: 'phase-advance',
    27	  GATE_ITERATION: 'gate-iteration',
    28	  PARK: 'park',
    29	  RESUME: 'resume',
    30	  SMOKE_AWAIT: 'smoke-await',
    31	  SMOKE_APPROVE: 'smoke-approve',
    32	});
    33	
    34	// Every gate loops at most 5 times — a repo-wide hard invariant (AGENTS.md
    35	// §Review gates); the reducer refuses a 6th and escalates instead.
    36	const GATE_ITERATION_CAP = 5;
    37	
    38	/** @param {string} root */
    39	export function loopPaths(root) {
    40	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff/loop`;
    41	  return { dir, state: `${dir}/state.json`, journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson` };
    42	}
    43	
    44	/**
    45	 * Fresh run state — pure and deterministic from the injected io (identity
    46	 * facts + clock only; no Date.now/Math.random, which would break replay).
    47	 * @param {any} spec a VALIDATED loop spec @param {any} io
    48	 */
    49	export function initLoopState(spec, io) {
    50	  const runId = `loop-${dedupeKey({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, at: io.now() }).slice(0, 12)}`;
    51	  /** @type {Record<string, number>} */
    52	  const iterations = {};
    53	  for (const p of spec.phases ?? []) {
    54	    if (/gate/.test(String(p.id))) iterations[p.id] = 0;
    55	  }
    56	  return {
    57	    schema: LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA,
    58	    runId,
    59	    goal: spec.goal,
    60	    phaseCount: Array.isArray(spec.phases) ? spec.phases.length : 0,
    61	    phaseIndex: 0,
    62	    iterations,
    63	    status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING,
    64	    parkReason: null,
    65	    escalation: null,
    66	    smokeApproval: null,
    67	    createdAt: io.now(),
    68	    journalSeq: 0,
    69	  };
    70	}
    71	
    72	/**
    73	 * The pure transition reducer. Never mutates its input; unknown events and
    74	 * refused transitions return the input state unchanged (a new reference is
    75	 * only produced for a real transition).
    76	 * @param {any} state @param {any} ev
    77	 */
    78	export function applyLoopEvent(state, ev) {
    79	  switch (ev?.type) {
    80	    case LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE: {
    81	      const next = state.phaseIndex + 1;
    82	      if (next >= state.phaseCount) return { ...state, phaseIndex: state.phaseCount, status: LOOP_STATUS.DONE };
    83	      return { ...state, phaseIndex: next };
    84	    }
    85	    case LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION: {
    86	      const gate = String(ev.gate);
    87	      const current = state.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0;
    88	      // The 6th attempt is REFUSED: the counter stays at the cap and the run
    89	      // escalates with a record naming the gate — never a 6th run.
    90	      if (current >= GATE_ITERATION_CAP) {
    91	        return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, escalation: { gate, iteration: current } };
    92	      }
    93	      return { ...state, iterations: { ...state.iterations, [gate]: current + 1 } };
    94	    }
    95	    case LOOP_EVENT.PARK:
    96	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, parkReason: ev.reason ?? null };
    97	    case LOOP_EVENT.RESUME:
    98	      // Only a PARK is resumable; an escalation is an operator decision.
    99	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) return state;
   100	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, parkReason: null };
   101	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT:
   102	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL };
   103	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE: {
   104	      // Guarded transition: only meaningful while awaiting, and only with the
   105	      // caller's verifySmokeToken result stamped verified === true. A missing
   106	      // or false `verified` is unverified — it never resumes the run.
   107	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) return state;
   108	      if (ev.verified !== true) return state;
   109	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, smokeApproval: { token: ev.token, at: ev.ts ?? null } };
   110	    }
   111	    default:
   112	      return state;
   113	  }
   114	}
   115	
   116	/**
   117	 * Atomic snapshot write (tmp + rename under .handoff/loop).
   118	 * @param {string} root @param {any} state @param {any} io
   119	 */
   120	export async function writeLoopState(root, state, io) {
   121	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   122	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   123	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, p.state, state);
   124	}
   125	
   126	/**
   127	 * Append one journal event; allocates the next seq past
   128	 * max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) unless the entry carries one.
   129	 * @param {string} root @param {any} ev @param {any} io
   130	 * @returns {Promise<number>} the persisted seq
   131	 */
   132	export async function appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io) {
   133	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   134	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   135	  let seq = ev.seq;
   136	  if (typeof seq !== 'number') {
   137	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.state);
   138	    const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   139	    let tailSeq = 0;
   140	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   141	      for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
   142	        if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
   143	      }
   144	    }
   145	    seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;
   146	  }
   147	  const ts = typeof ev.ts === 'string' ? ev.ts : io.now();
   148	  const entry = {
   149	    ...ev,
   150	    seq,
   151	    ts,
   152	    dedupeKey: typeof ev.dedupeKey === 'string' && ev.dedupeKey.length > 0 ? ev.dedupeKey : dedupeKey({ seq, ts, type: ev.type, gate: ev.gate ?? null }),
   153	  };
   154	  appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, entry);
   155	  return seq;
   156	}
   157	
   158	/**
   159	 * Load = snapshot + torn-tail-tolerant replay of journal events PAST the
   160	 * snapshot's seq. Duplicate seqs/dedupeKeys apply exactly once, so a
   161	 * supervisor-death recovery never re-runs a completed iteration.
   162	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   163	 * @returns {Promise<{state: any, warnings: string[]}>}
   164	 */
   165	export async function loadLoopState(root, io) {
   166	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   167	  /** @type {string[]} */
   168	  const warnings = [];
   169	  const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.state);
   170	  if (!snap.ok) return { state: null, warnings: ['no loop state snapshot found'] };
   171	  let state = snap.value;
   172	  const baseSeq = typeof state.journalSeq === 'number' ? state.journalSeq : 0;
   173	
   174	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   175	    const { entries, warnings: jw } = readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal);
   176	    for (const w of jw ?? []) warnings.push(`journal: ${w.kind ?? 'warning'} at line ${w.line ?? '?'}`);
   177	    const applied = new Set();
   178	    let maxSeq = baseSeq;
   179	    for (const e of entries) {
   180	      const seq = typeof e.seq === 'number' ? e.seq : null;
   181	      if (seq !== null && seq <= baseSeq) continue; // already folded into the snapshot
   182	      const key = e.dedupeKey ?? (seq !== null ? `seq:${seq}` : null);
   183	      if (key !== null && applied.has(key)) continue; // replayed duplicate — apply once
   184	      if (seq !== null && applied.has(`seq:${seq}`)) continue;
   185	      state = applyLoopEvent(state, e);
   186	      if (key !== null) applied.add(key);
   187	      if (seq !== null) {
   188	        applied.add(`seq:${seq}`);
   189	        if (seq > maxSeq) maxSeq = seq;
   190	      }
   191	    }
   192	    state = { ...state, journalSeq: maxSeq };
   193	  }
   194	  return { state, warnings };
   195	}
   196	
   197	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   198	// Smoke-approval token — a STRUCTURED digest over the six bound inputs, so a
   199	// stale approval is refused NAMING what moved (mirrors the receive receipt
   200	// token's driftedInputs discipline).
   201	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   202	
   203	const SMOKE_TOKEN_PREFIX = 'smk1';
   204	const SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS = /** @type {const} */ ([
   205	  'stateDigest',
   206	  'smokeCmd',
   207	  'smokeOutputDigest',
   208	  'gitHead',
   209	  'gitContentDigest',
   210	  'childAssignment',
   211	]);
   212	
   213	/**
   214	 * Deterministic structured token. Per-field digests keep field boundaries
   215	 * (no concatenation collisions) and let verification name drifted inputs.
   216	 * Missing bound fields throw — a token over partial inputs is meaningless.
   217	 * @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
   218	 * @returns {string}
   219	 */
   220	export function smokeApprovalToken(inputs) {
   221	  for (const f of SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS) {
   222	    if (typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') throw new Error(`smoke approval token: required input '${f}' is missing`);
   223	  }
   224	  return `${SMOKE_TOKEN_PREFIX}.` + SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.map((f) => dedupeKey(inputs[f]).slice(0, 10)).join('.');
   225	}
   226	
   227	/**
   228	 * Recompute against current inputs; positional comparison names every
   229	 * drifted input. Never throws — partial current inputs simply cannot verify.
   230	 * @param {string} presented @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
   231	 * @returns {{ok: true} | {ok: false, driftedInputs: string[]}}
   232	 */
   233	export function verifySmokeToken(presented, inputs) {
   234	  /** @type {string} */
   235	  let current;
   236	  try {
   237	    current = smokeApprovalToken(inputs);
   238	  } catch {
   239	    return { ok: false, driftedInputs: SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.filter((f) => typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') };
   240	  }
   241	  if (presented === current) return { ok: true };
   242	  const a = String(presented ?? '').split('.');
   243	  const b = current.split('.');
   244	  /** @type {string[]} */
   245	  const drifted = [];
   246	  SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.forEach((f, i) => {
   247	    if (a[i + 1] !== b[i + 1]) drifted.push(f);
   248	  });
   249	  return { ok: false, driftedInputs: drifted.length > 0 ? drifted : [...SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS] };
   250	}

    30	/** @param {string} root */
    31	export function worktreePaths(root) {

    33	  /** @type {Record<string, string>} */
    34	  const seats = { a: `${dir}/wt-a`, b: `${dir}/wt-b` };
    35	  return { dir, seats };

    39	const seatBranch = (seat) => `baton/wt-${seat}/base`;

    41	/** @param {any} io @param {string} cwd @param {string[]} args */
    42	async function git(io, cwd, args) {
    43	  return io.execFile('git', args, { cwd });
    44	}

    46	/** Parse `git worktree list --porcelain` into [{path, branch}]. @param {string} out */
    47	function parseWorktreeList(out) {
    48	  /** @type {Array<{path: string, branch: string | null}>} */
    49	  const entries = [];
    50	  for (const block of String(out).split(/\n\n+/)) {
    51	    const path = block.match(/^worktree (.+)$/m)?.[1];
    52	    if (!path) continue;
    53	    const branch = block.match(/^branch refs\/heads\/(.+)$/m)?.[1] ?? null;
    54	    entries.push({ path, branch });
    55	  }
    56	  return entries;
    57	}
    58	
    59	/**
    60	 * Ensure both seat worktrees exist on their namespaced branches and that
    61	 * `.worktrees/` is gitignored. Idempotent: an existing seat is left alone.
    62	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
    63	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, worktrees: Array<{seat: string, path: string, branch: string}>, gitignoreChanged: boolean}>}
    64	 */
    65	export async function setupWorktrees(root, io) {
    66	  const p = worktreePaths(root);
    67	
    68	  const gitignorePath = `${root}/.gitignore`;
    69	  const existing = io.fs.existsSync(gitignorePath) ? io.fs.readFileSync(gitignorePath, 'utf8') : '';
    70	  const ensured = ensureIgnoreLine(existing, '.worktrees/');
    71	  if (ensured.changed) io.fs.writeFileSync(gitignorePath, ensured.text);
    72	
    73	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
    74	  /** @type {Array<{seat: string, path: string, branch: string}>} */
    75	  const worktrees = [];
    76	  for (const seat of SEATS) {
    77	    const path = p.seats[seat];
    78	    const present = listed.find((e) => e.path === path);
    79	    if (present) {
    80	      worktrees.push({ seat, path, branch: present.branch ?? seatBranch(seat) });
    81	      continue;
    82	    }
    83	    const branch = seatBranch(seat);
    84	    await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'add', '-b', branch, path]);
    85	    worktrees.push({ seat, path, branch });
    86	  }
    87	  return { ok: true, worktrees, gitignoreChanged: ensured.changed };
    88	}
    89	
    90	/**
    91	 * Preflight before a child runs in a seat: the worktree is listed, its index
    92	 * is clean, and HEAD sits on the expected branch. Refusals name the failed
    93	 * check and never issue destructive git.
    94	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string}} target @param {any} io
    95	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, check: string, refusal: string}>}
    96	 */
    97	export async function preflightWorktree(root, target, io) {
    98	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
    99	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
   100	  if (!listed.some((e) => e.path === path)) {
   101	    return { ok: false, check: 'missing-worktree', refusal: `worktree ${path} is not listed by git — run setup or self-heal first` };
   102	  }
   103	  const status = (await git(io, path, ['status', '--porcelain'])).stdout.trim();
   104	  if (status.length > 0) {
   105	    return { ok: false, check: 'dirty-index', refusal: `worktree ${path} has a dirty index/working tree — a clean seat is required before a child runs` };
   106	  }
   107	  const head = (await git(io, path, ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'])).stdout.trim();
   108	  if (head !== target.branch) {
   109	    return { ok: false, check: 'wrong-branch', refusal: `worktree ${path} HEAD is on '${head}', expected branch '${target.branch}'` };
   110	  }
   111	  return { ok: true };
   112	}
   113	
   114	/**
   115	 * Postflight after a child: HEAD still on the expected branch (checked in the
   116	 * seat cwd) and main unmoved from the pre-child capture.
   117	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string, mainSha: string}} target @param {any} io
   118	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, check: string, refusal: string}>}
   119	 */
   120	export async function postflightWorktree(root, target, io) {
   121	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
   122	  const head = (await git(io, path, ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'])).stdout.trim();
   123	  if (head !== target.branch) {
   124	    return { ok: false, check: 'head-drift', refusal: `the child left HEAD on '${head}' instead of the expected branch '${target.branch}'` };
   125	  }
   126	  const main = (await git(io, root, ['rev-parse', 'main'])).stdout.trim();
   127	  if (main !== target.mainSha) {
   128	    return { ok: false, check: 'main-moved', refusal: `main moved under the child (was ${target.mainSha}, now ${main}) — only the merger writes main` };
   129	  }
   130	  return { ok: true };
   131	}
   132	
   133	/**
   134	 * Full-range attribution scan over main..branch — the merge gate. Every
   135	 * commit must be authored AND committed by the sole author, with no forbidden
   136	 * AI-attribution trailer in the body. The range is never windowed.
   137	 * @param {string} root @param {string} branch @param {any} io
   138	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, offending: string, reason: string}>}
   139	 */
   140	export async function attributionScan(root, branch, io) {
   141	  const out = (await git(io, root, ['log', `main..${branch}`, `--format=${LOG_FORMAT}`])).stdout;
   142	  for (const record of String(out).split('\x1e')) {
   143	    if (record.trim().length === 0) continue;
   144	    const [sha, an, ae, cn, ce, body = ''] = record.replace(/^\n/, '').split('\x00');
   145	    if (an !== SOLE_AUTHOR.name || ae !== SOLE_AUTHOR.email) {
   146	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} has a foreign author identity (${an} <${ae}>) — sole-author is required` };
   147	    }
   148	    if (cn !== SOLE_AUTHOR.name || ce !== SOLE_AUTHOR.email) {
   149	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} has a foreign committer identity (${cn} <${ce}>) — sole-author is required` };
   150	    }
   151	    if (TRAILER_PATTERNS.some((re) => re.test(body))) {
   152	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} carries a forbidden AI-attribution trailer (co-authored/generated-with/emoji) in its message` };
   153	    }
   154	  }
   155	  return { ok: true };
   156	}
   157	
   158	const mergeLockPath = (/** @type {string} */ root) => `${root}/.handoff/loop/merge.lock`;
   159	
   160	/**
   161	 * Merge an approved subtask branch into main — one guarded transaction:
   162	 * acquire the merge lock (a held lock refuses), gate on the attribution scan,
   163	 * merge at the repo root, abort-and-park on conflict, then ff-only sync both
   164	 * seats. The lock is released on EVERY exit path after acquisition.
   165	 * @param {string} root @param {{branch: string}} input @param {any} io
   166	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true, branch: string} | {ok: false, reason: string}>}
   167	 */
   168	export async function mergeSubtask(root, input, io) {
   169	  const lock = mergeLockPath(root);
   170	  if (io.fs.existsSync(lock)) {
   171	    return { ok: false, reason: `the merge lock at ${lock} is held by another merger — refusing a concurrent merge` };
   172	  }
   173	  ensureDir(io.fs, `${root}/.handoff/loop`);
   174	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, lock, { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, at: io.now() });
   175	  try {
   176	    const scan = await attributionScan(root, input.branch, io);
   177	    if (scan.ok !== true) return { ok: false, reason: scan.reason };
   178	
   179	    try {
   180	      await git(io, root, ['merge', input.branch]);
   181	    } catch (err) {
   182	      // Conflict (or any merge failure): abort, never auto-resolve — park.
   183	      try {
   184	        await git(io, root, ['merge', '--abort']);
   185	      } catch {
   186	        // Nothing to abort — the failure predates a merge state.
   187	      }
   188	      const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.stdout || /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message || String(err);
   189	      return { ok: false, reason: `merge conflict — aborted and parked for the operator: ${String(msg).split('\n')[0]}` };
   190	    }
   191	
   192	    const seats = worktreePaths(root).seats;
   193	    for (const seat of SEATS) {
   194	      try {
   195	        await git(io, seats[seat], ['merge', '--ff-only', 'main']);
   196	      } catch (err) {
   197	        const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.stderr || /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message || String(err);
   198	        return { ok: false, reason: `worktree wt-${seat} could not fast-forward to main — corruption signal, parked: ${String(msg).split('\n')[0]}` };
   199	      }
   200	    }
   201	    return { ok: true, branch: input.branch };
   202	  } finally {
   203	    try {
   204	      if (io.fs.existsSync(lock)) io.fs.unlinkSync(lock);
   205	    } catch {
   206	      // Best effort — a leaked lock is visible and operator-recoverable.
   207	    }
   208	  }
   209	}
   210	
   211	/**
   212	 * Self-heal a raw-deleted seat: git still lists it but the directory is gone
   213	 * — prune the stale metadata and re-add on the same branch.
   214	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string}} target @param {any} io
   215	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, healed: boolean}>}
   216	 */
   217	export async function selfHealWorktree(root, target, io) {
   218	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
   219	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
   220	  const stale = listed.some((e) => e.path === path) && !io.fs.existsSync(path);
   221	  if (!stale) return { ok: true, healed: false };
   222	  await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'prune']);
   223	  await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'add', path, target.branch]);
   224	  return { ok: true, healed: true };
   225	}
   226	
   227	/**
   228	 * Tear down BOTH seats: remove the worktrees and delete their namespaced
   229	 * branches. Anything outside .worktrees/wt-* or baton/wt-* is never touched,
   230	 * so a teardown leaves the repo a plain git repo (acceptance constraint 5).
   231	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   232	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, removed: string[]}>}
   233	 */
   234	export async function teardownWorktrees(root, io) {
   235	  const p = worktreePaths(root);
   236	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
   237	  /** @type {string[]} */
   238	  const removed = [];
   239	  for (const seat of SEATS) {
   240	    const entry = listed.find((e) => e.path === p.seats[seat]);
   241	    if (!entry) continue;
   242	    await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'remove', '--force', p.seats[seat]]);
   243	    // Delete the seat's CURRENT branch and its setup base branch — a seat
   244	    // that moved on to subtask branches orphans its base otherwise
   245	    // (acceptance constraint 5: teardown leaves a plain git repo). Only
   246	    // namespaced branches are ever deleted.
   247	    /** @type {Set<string>} */
   248	    const toDelete = new Set();
   249	    if (entry.branch && WORKTREE_BRANCH_RE.test(entry.branch)) toDelete.add(entry.branch);
   250	    toDelete.add(seatBranch(seat));
   251	    for (const branch of toDelete) {
   252	      if (!WORKTREE_BRANCH_RE.test(branch)) continue;
   253	      try {
   254	        await git(io, root, ['branch', '-D', branch]);
   255	      } catch {
   256	        // Already gone (current === base, or deleted earlier) — fine.
   257	      }
   258	    }
   259	    removed.push(seat);
   260	  }
   261	  return { ok: true, removed };
   262	}
   263	
   264	/**
   265	 * Branch jail: only baton/wt-* branches may ever be deleted through this
   266	 * layer. An out-of-namespace ref refuses without touching git.
   267	 * @param {string} root @param {string} branch @param {any} io
   268	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, error: string}>}
   269	 */
   270	export async function deleteBranch(root, branch, io) {
   271	  if (!WORKTREE_BRANCH_RE.test(branch)) {
   272	    return { ok: false, error: `refused: '${branch}' is outside the baton/wt- namespace — the loop never deletes non-loop branches` };
   273	  }
   274	  await git(io, root, ['branch', '-D', branch]);
   275	  return { ok: true };
   276	}

     2	// { cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now }. Backed by memfs.

     5	// execResults map keyed by the joined command string.
     6	//
     7	// Wave-B2 additions (bundle lock layer) — all optional, defaulted, and purely
     8	// additive so no prior consumer is affected:
     9	//   host / pid / startTime  — the process-identity facts the lock model writes
    10	//     into owner.json and compares on contention (see plan §Concurrency, Lock
    11	//     model iterations 3+4).
    12	//   processAlive(pid, startTime) — the "provably dead" oracle. Default: only
    13	//     THIS io's own {pid, startTime} is alive; every other descriptor reads as
    14	//     dead. Override per test to model a live foreign owner or a pid/start-time
    15	//     mismatch (reused pid).
    16	//   newFencingToken() — the injected per-acquisition token source. Deterministic
    17	//     monotonic counter ("tok-1", "tok-2", …), matching the injected-io
    18	//     discipline already used for now()/execFile so lock acquisition is
    19	//     reproducible in tests rather than drawing from Math.random.
    20	
    21	import { makeMemfs } from './memfs.mjs';
    22	
    23	export function makeIo({
    24	  files = {},
    25	  env = {},
    26	  stdin = '',
    27	  execResults = {},
    28	  now = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z',
    29	  host = 'host-A',
    30	  pid = 4242,
    31	  startTime = 111000,
    32	  processAlive = null,
    33	  fencingSeed = 1,
    34	  execPath = process.execPath,
    35	} = {}) {
    36	  const { fs, files: filesSnapshot } = makeMemfs(files);
    37	  const outChunks = [];
    38	  const errChunks = [];
    39	  let currentNow = now;
    40	  let tokenCounter = fencingSeed - 1;
    41	  const defaultAlive = (p, s) => p === pid && s === startTime;
    42	
    43	  const stdout = { write: (s) => { outChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
    44	  const stderr = { write: (s) => { errChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
    45	
    46	  const execFile = (cmd, args = [], /* opts */ _opts = {}) => {
    47	    const key = [cmd, ...(Array.isArray(args) ? args : [])].join(' ');
    48	    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    49	      if (!(key in execResults)) {
    50	        const e = new Error(`spawn ${cmd} ENOENT`);
    51	        e.code = 'ENOENT';
    52	        return reject(e);
    53	      }
    54	      const r = execResults[key];
    55	      const code = r.code ?? 0;
    56	      const out = r.stdout ?? '';
    57	      const err = r.stderr ?? '';
    58	      if (code !== 0) {
    59	        // util.promisify(execFile) rejects on nonzero exit; error carries code + streams.
    60	        const e = new Error(`Command failed: ${key}`);
    61	        e.code = code;
    62	        e.stdout = out;
    63	        e.stderr = err;
    64	        return reject(e);
    65	      }
    66	      resolve({ stdout: out, stderr: err });
    67	    });
    68	  };
    69	
    70	  return {
    71	    cwd: '/repo',
    72	    env,
    73	    stdin,
    74	    stdout,
    75	    stderr,
    76	    fs,
    77	    execFile,
    78	    now: () => currentNow,
    79	    setNow: (iso) => { currentNow = iso; },
    80	    execPath,
    81	    host,
    82	    pid,
    83	    startTime,
    84	    processAlive: processAlive || defaultAlive,
    85	    // Tiny retry budget: unit tests exercising live-lock refusal stay fast
    86	    // while the retry code path itself still runs (production default: 120x25ms).
    87	    lockRetry: { attempts: 2, delayMs: 2 },
    88	    newFencingToken: () => `tok-${++tokenCounter}`,
    89	    stdoutText: () => outChunks.join(''),
    90	    stderrText: () => errChunks.join(''),
    91	    files: filesSnapshot,
    92	  };
    93	}

     1	/**
     2	 * Filesystem primitives with crash-safety discipline.
     3	 * All functions take an injected fs-like object so tests run on a memfs fake.
     4	 */
     5	
     6	let tmpCounter = 0;
     7	
     8	/** @param {string} path */
     9	function tmpPathFor(path) {
    10	  tmpCounter += 1;
    11	  return `${path}.tmp.${process.pid}-${tmpCounter}`;
    12	}
    13	
    14	/**
    15	 * Rename with retry on EPERM (Windows AV/indexer lock pattern): 3 attempts total.
    16	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} from @param {string} to
    17	 */
    18	function renameWithRetry(fs, from, to) {
    19	  let lastErr;
    20	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt += 1) {
    21	    try {
    22	      fs.renameSync(from, to);
    23	      return;
    24	    } catch (err) {
    25	      lastErr = err;
    26	      if (!err || /** @type {any} */ (err).code !== 'EPERM') throw err;
    27	    }
    28	  }
    29	  throw lastErr;
    30	}
    31	
    32	/**
    33	 * Write text atomically: tmp sibling + rename. The live target is never
    34	 * truncated or partially written.
    35	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {string} content
    36	 */
    37	export function atomicWriteText(fs, path, content) {
    38	  const tmp = tmpPathFor(path);
    39	  fs.writeFileSync(tmp, content);
    40	  try {
    41	    renameWithRetry(fs, tmp, path);
    42	  } catch (err) {
    43	    try {
    44	      fs.unlinkSync(tmp);
    45	    } catch {
    46	      // leftover tmp is harmless; cleanupTmp removes it later
    47	    }
    48	    throw err;
    49	  }
    50	}
    51	
    52	/**
    53	 * Atomically write pretty-printed JSON (2-space, trailing newline).
    54	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {unknown} obj
    55	 */
    56	export function atomicWriteJson(fs, path, obj) {
    57	  atomicWriteText(fs, path, JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2) + '\n');
    58	}
    59	
    60	/**
    61	 * Read + parse JSON without ever throwing.
    62	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path
    63	 * @returns {{ok: true, value: any} | {ok: false, error: string}}
    64	 */
    65	export function safeReadJson(fs, path) {
    66	  let raw;
    67	  try {
    68	    raw = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
    69	  } catch (err) {
    70	    return { ok: false, error: `read failed: ${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code ?? err}` };
    71	  }
    72	  try {
    73	    return { ok: true, value: JSON.parse(raw) };
    74	  } catch (err) {
    75	    return { ok: false, error: `parse failed: ${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? err}` };
    76	  }
    77	}
    78	
    79	/**
    80	 * Copy the current content to <path>.bak (when present), then write atomically.
    81	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {string} content
    82	 */
    83	export function backupThenWrite(fs, path, content) {
    84	  if (fs.existsSync(path)) {
    85	    fs.copyFileSync(path, `${path}.bak`);
    86	  }
    87	  atomicWriteText(fs, path, content);
    88	}
    89	
    90	/**
    91	 * Remove leftover *.tmp.* files in a directory (crash debris).
    92	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} dir
    93	 */
    94	export function cleanupTmp(fs, dir) {
    95	  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return;
    96	  for (const name of fs.readdirSync(dir)) {
    97	    if (/\.tmp\./.test(name)) {
    98	      try {
    99	        fs.unlinkSync(`${dir}/${name}`);
   100	      } catch {
   101	        // already gone or locked; not fatal
   102	      }
   103	    }
   104	  }
   105	}
   106	
   107	/**
   108	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path
   109	 */
   110	export function ensureDir(fs, path) {
   111	  fs.mkdirSync(path, { recursive: true });
   112	}

   225	  // A scripted loop runner: returns the next result and writes its log so the
   226	  // supervisor classifies the transcript from the child's LOG file.
   227	  function scriptedRunner(script) {
   228	    const queue = [...script];
   229	    const calls = [];
   230	    const fn = async (childSpec, opts) => {
   231	      calls.push({ command: childSpec.command, args: (childSpec.args ?? []).map(String) });
   232	      const r = queue.shift() ?? { verdict: 'APPROVED' };
   233	      if (typeof r.logContent === 'string' && opts.logPath) {
   234	        nodeFs.mkdirSync(opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/')), { recursive: true });
   235	        nodeFs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, r.logContent);
   236	      }
   237	      return { timedOut: false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath };
   238	    };
   239	    fn.calls = calls;
   240	    return fn;
   241	  }
   242
