  2644	     1	/**
  2645	     2	 * Child supervision — the loop's reliability surface. Owns the FULL child
  2646	     3	 * lifecycle: argv assembly (per-role permissions), detached process-group
  2647	     4	 * spawn with closed stdin, timeout SIGTERM→grace→SIGKILL of the whole group,
  2648	     5	 * capped transcript logs, region-bounded verdict parsing (prompt-echo-proof),
  2649	     6	 * and exit classification via core detect. Plan:
  2650	     7	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"Child supervision
  2651	     8	 * contract".
  2652	     9	 */
  2653	    10	import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
  2654	    11	import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
  2655	    12	import { dirname } from 'node:path';
  2656	    13	import { classify } from '../detect/classifier.mjs';
  2657	    14	
  2658	    15	// Every review seat is read-only — writers are the implementer-side roles
  2659	    16	// only. The merger CHILD is read-only too: it adversarially re-checks, but
  2660	    17	// the SUPERVISOR performs the actual merge via worktrees.mergeSubtask
  2661	    18	// (pipeline contract — the merger child never issues git).
  2662	    19	const REVIEWER_ROLES = new Set(['plan-reviewer', 'test-verifier', 'subtask-reviewer', 'final-reviewer-a', 'final-reviewer-b', 'merger']);
  2663	    20	
  2664	    21	// AGENTS.md §9: children commit solely as the repo owner, zero AI attribution.
  2665	    22	const GIT_IDENTITY = Object.freeze({
  2666	    23	  GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: 'SakshamUboweja',
  2667	    24	  GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: 'ssakshamu@gmail.com',
  2668	    25	  GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: 'SakshamUboweja',
  2669	    26	  GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: 'ssakshamu@gmail.com',
  2670	    27	});
  2671	    28	
  2672	    29	/**
  2673	    30	 * Assemble the spawn contract for a role child. Reviewers are read-only on
  2674	    31	 * every platform; every child is marked supervised (its own baton hooks
  2675	    32	 * no-op), carries the sole-author git identity, and has stdin CLOSED at
  2676	    33	 * spawn (the codex-exec stdin-hang field failure).
  2677	    34	 * @param {{platform: string, role: string, model: string, effort?: string | null, mode?: string}} assignment
  2678	    35	 * @param {string} prompt
  2679	    36	 * @param {{root: string}} opts
  2680	    37	 * @returns {{command: string, args: string[], env: Record<string, string>, stdio: any[]}}
  2681	    38	 */
  2682	    39	export function buildChildArgv(assignment, prompt, opts) {
  2683	    40	  const readOnly = REVIEWER_ROLES.has(assignment.role);
  2684	    41	  const env = { BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD: '1', ...GIT_IDENTITY };
  2685	    42	  const stdio = ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'];
  2686	    43	
  2687	    44	  if (assignment.platform === 'claude-code') {
  2688	    45	    const args = ['-p', prompt];
  2689	    46	    if (readOnly) {
  2690	    47	      args.push('--permission-mode', 'default', '--allowedTools', 'Read,Grep,Glob');
  2691	    48	    } else {
  2692	    49	      args.push('--permission-mode', 'acceptEdits', '--allowedTools', 'Read,Grep,Glob,Write,Edit,Bash');
  2693	    50	    }
  2694	    51	    return { command: 'claude', args, env, stdio };
  2695	    52	  }
  2696	    53	
  2697	    54	  // codex (and the default shape for exec-style CLIs).
  2698	    55	  const args = ['exec', '-C', opts.root, '-s', readOnly ? 'read-only' : 'workspace-write', '--model', assignment.model];
  2699	    56	  if (assignment.effort) args.push('-c', `model_reasoning_effort=${assignment.effort}`);
  2700	    57	  args.push(prompt);
  2701	    58	  return { command: 'codex', args, env, stdio };
  2702	    59	}
  2703	    60	
  2704	    61	const VERDICT_RE = /VERDICT:\s*(APPROVED_WITH_NOTES|APPROVED|BLOCKED)\b/g;
  2705	    62	
  2706	    63	/** @param {string} region @returns {{verdict: string, findings: string} | null} */
  2707	    64	function lastVerdictIn(region) {
  2708	    65	  let last = null;
  2709	    66	  for (const m of region.matchAll(VERDICT_RE)) last = m;
  2710	    67	  if (!last) return null;
  2711	    68	  const after = region.slice((last.index ?? 0) + last[0].length);
  2712	    69	  return { verdict: last[1], findings: after.trim() };
  2713	    70	}
  2714	    71	
  2715	    72	/**
  2716	    73	 * Region-bounded verdict parsing — the prompt region (which quotes the
  2717	    74	 * verdict format in its instructions) is NEVER scanned, and there is no
  2718	    75	 * whole-transcript fallback. Unparseable is BLOCKED, never APPROVED.
  2719	    76	 * - codex: only the text after the LAST line starting with 'tokens used'.
  2720	    77	 * - claude-code: only the `result` field of the structured JSON output.
  2721	    78	 * Multiple tails inside the trusted region → the LAST one wins.
  2722	    79	 * @param {string} transcript @param {{platform: string}} opts
  2723	    80	 * @returns {{verdict: string, findings: string, reason?: string}}
  2724	    81	 */
  2725	    82	export function parseVerdict(transcript, { platform }) {
  2726	    83	  const unparseable = { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: '', reason: 'unparseable' };
  2727	    84	  const text = String(transcript ?? '');
  2728	    85	
  2729	    86	  if (platform === 'claude-code') {
  2730	    87	    let obj = null;
  2731	    88	    try {
  2732	    89	      obj = JSON.parse(text.trim());
  2733	    90	    } catch {
  2734	    91	      return unparseable;
  2735	    92	    }
  2736	    93	    if (typeof obj?.result !== 'string') return unparseable;
  2737	    94	    const hit = lastVerdictIn(obj.result);
  2738	    95	    return hit ?? unparseable;
  2739	    96	  }
  2740	    97	
  2741	    98	  // codex: locate the LAST 'tokens used' marker line; the trusted region is
  2742	    99	  // strictly after it.
  2743	   100	  const lines = text.split('\n');
  2744	   101	  let markerLine = -1;
  2745	   102	  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
  2746	   103	    if (/^tokens used\b/i.test(lines[i])) markerLine = i;
  2747	   104	  }
  2748	   105	  if (markerLine === -1) return unparseable;
  2749	   106	  const region = lines.slice(markerLine + 1).join('\n');
  2750	   107	  const hit = lastVerdictIn(region);
  2751	   108	  return hit ?? unparseable;
  2752	   109	}
  2753	   110	
  2754	   111	const TRUNCATION_MARKER = '\n[... truncated by baton log cap ...]\n';
  2755	   112	
  2756	   113	/**
  2757	   114	 * Cap a transcript: head preserved, middle replaced with a marker, TAIL
  2758	   115	 * preserved verbatim (the verdict lives at the end and must never be cut).
  2759	   116	 * @param {string} text @param {number} maxBytes
  2760	   117	 * @returns {string}
  2761	   118	 */
  2762	   119	export function capLog(text, maxBytes) {
  2763	   120	  const s = String(text ?? '');
  2764	   121	  if (Buffer.byteLength(s) <= maxBytes) return s;
  2765	   122	  const headBytes = Math.max(1, Math.floor(maxBytes * 0.4));
  2766	   123	  const tailBytes = Math.max(1, maxBytes - headBytes);
  2767	   124	  const buf = Buffer.from(s);
  2768	   125	  const head = buf.subarray(0, headBytes).toString();
  2769	   126	  const tail = buf.subarray(buf.length - tailBytes).toString();
  2770	   127	  return head + TRUNCATION_MARKER + tail;
  2771	   128	}
  2772	   129	
  2773	   130	/**
  2774	   131	 * Exit classification delegates to core detect — one classifier, one
  2775	   132	 * signature table, no drift. Returns exactly classify(...).class.
  2776	   133	 * @param {string} transcript @param {number} exitCode @param {string} platform @param {any} table
  2777	   134	 * @returns {string}
  2778	   135	 */
  2779	   136	export function classifyChildExit(transcript, exitCode, platform, table) {
  2780	   137	  return classify({ text: transcript, exitCode, platform, table }).class;
  2781	   138	}
  2782	   139	
  2783	   140	/** @param {number} pid @param {NodeJS.Signals} sig */
  2784	   141	function killGroup(pid, sig) {
  2785	   142	  try {
  2786	   143	    process.kill(-pid, sig);
  2787	   144	  } catch {
  2788	   145	    // ESRCH — already gone.
  2789	   146	  }
  2790	   147	}
  2791	   148	
  2792	   149	/**
  2793	   150	 * Spawn and supervise one child: detached in its OWN process group, stdin
  2794	   151	 * closed; on timeout SIGTERM the group, wait graceMs, SIGKILL the group (no
  2795	   152	 * zombie grandchildren). Output streams to a capped log at opts.logPath; the
  2796	   153	 * verdict is parsed from the full transcript, region-bounded.
  2797	   154	 * @param {{command: string, args: string[], env?: Record<string, string>, cwd?: string}} spec
  2798	   155	 * @param {{timeoutMs: number, graceMs: number, logPath: string, maxLogBytes?: number, platform?: string}} opts
  2799	   156	 * @returns {Promise<{timedOut: boolean, exitCode: number | null, verdict?: string, findings?: string, reason?: string, logPath: string}>}
  2800	   157	 */
  2801	   158	export function superviseChild(spec, opts) {
  2802	   159	  return new Promise((resolve) => {
  2803	   160	    const maxLogBytes = opts.maxLogBytes ?? 1_000_000;
  2804	   161	    const child = spawn(spec.command, spec.args, {
  2805	   162	      cwd: spec.cwd,
  2806	   163	      env: { ...process.env, ...(spec.env ?? {}) },
  2807	   164	      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
  2808	   165	      detached: true, // its own process group — the kill unit
  2809	   166	    });
  2810	   167	
  2811	   168	    let out = '';
  2812	   169	    let timedOut = false;
  2813	   170	    /** @type {NodeJS.Timeout | null} */
  2814	   171	    let killTimer = null;
  2815	   172	
  2816	   173	    const onChunk = (/** @type {Buffer} */ c) => {
  2817	   174	      out += c.toString();
  2818	   175	    };
  2819	   176	    child.stdout?.on('data', onChunk);
  2820	   177	    child.stderr?.on('data', onChunk);
  2821	   178	
  2822	   179	    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
  2823	   180	      timedOut = true;
  2824	   181	      if (typeof child.pid === 'number') {
  2825	   182	        killGroup(child.pid, 'SIGTERM');
  2826	   183	        killTimer = setTimeout(() => {
  2827	   184	          if (typeof child.pid === 'number') killGroup(child.pid, 'SIGKILL');
  2828	   185	        }, opts.graceMs);
  2829	   186	      }
  2830	   187	    }, opts.timeoutMs);
  2831	   188	
  2832	   189	    const finish = (/** @type {number | null} */ exitCode) => {
  2833	   190	      clearTimeout(timeout);
  2834	   191	      if (killTimer) clearTimeout(killTimer);
  2835	   192	      // Belt-and-braces: reap any group survivors even on a clean exit path.
  2836	   193	      if (timedOut && typeof child.pid === 'number') killGroup(child.pid, 'SIGKILL');
  2837	   194	      try {
  2838	   195	        mkdirSync(dirname(opts.logPath), { recursive: true });
  2839	   196	        writeFileSync(opts.logPath, capLog(out, maxLogBytes));
  2840	   197	      } catch {
  2841	   198	        // The log must never mask the result.
  2842	   199	      }
  2843	   200	      const parsed = parseVerdict(out, { platform: opts.platform ?? 'codex' });
  2844	   201	      resolve({
  2845	   202	        timedOut,
  2846	   203	        exitCode: timedOut ? null : exitCode,
  2847	   204	        verdict: parsed.verdict,
  2848	   205	        findings: parsed.findings,
  2849	   206	        ...(parsed.reason ? { reason: parsed.reason } : {}),
  2850	   207	        logPath: opts.logPath,
  2851	   208	      });
  2852	   209	    };
  2853	   210	
  2854	   211	    child.on('error', () => finish(null));
  2855	   212	    child.on('close', (code) => finish(code));
  2856	   213	  });
  2857	   214	}

  2946	    31	/**
  2947	    32	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
  2948	    33	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
  2949	    34	 */
  2950	    35	export async function cmdPipeline(args, io) {
  2951	    36	  /** @type {string[]} */
  2952	    37	  const positionals = [];
  2953	    38	  /** @type {string[]} */
  2954	    39	  const flagTokens = [];
  2955	    40	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
  2956	    41	    const a = args[i];
  2957	    42	    if (typeof a === 'string' && a.startsWith('--')) {
  2958	    43	      flagTokens.push(a);
  2959	    44	      if (STRING_FLAGS.has(a.slice(2)) && i + 1 < args.length) {
  2960	    45	        i += 1;
  2961	    46	        flagTokens.push(args[i]);
  2962	    47	      }
  2963	    48	    } else {
  2964	    49	      positionals.push(a);
  2965	    50	    }
  2966	    51	  }
  2967	    52	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(flagTokens, {});
  2968	    53	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'pipeline', parsed.error);
  2969	    54	  const flags = parsed.flags;
  2970	    55	
  2971	    56	  const sub = positionals[0];
  2972	    57	  if (sub === undefined) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', 'a subcommand is required — try: baton pipeline run');
  2973	    58	  if (sub !== 'run') return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `unknown subcommand '${sub}' (supported: run)`);
  2974	    59	  if (positionals.length > 1) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `unexpected argument '${positionals[1]}'`);
  2975	    60	
  2976	    61	  return runPipeline(flags, io);
  2977	    62	}
  2978	    63	
  2979	    64	/** Validate the spec's subtasks — every rejection names its offender.
  2980	    65	 * @param {any} subtasks @returns {string | null} */
  2981	    66	function validateSubtasks(subtasks) {
  2982	    67	  if (!Array.isArray(subtasks) || subtasks.length === 0) {
  2983	    68	    return 'loop.json needs a non-empty `subtasks` array ([{id, title}]) for a pipeline run';
  2984	    69	  }
  2985	    70	  const seen = new Set();
  2986	    71	  for (let i = 0; i < subtasks.length; i += 1) {
  2987	    72	    const st = subtasks[i];
  2988	    73	    if (typeof st?.id !== 'string' || st.id.length === 0) return `subtasks[${i}] is missing a string id`;
  2989	    74	    if (typeof st?.title !== 'string' || st.title.length === 0) return `subtasks[${i}] ('${st.id}') is missing a string title`;
  2990	    75	    if (seen.has(st.id)) return `subtasks carry a duplicate id '${st.id}' — ids name branches and must be unique`;
  2991	    76	    seen.add(st.id);
  2992	    77	  }
  2993	    78	  return null;
  2994	    79	}

  2996	    81	/**
  2997	    82	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags @param {any} io

  2999	    84	 */
  3000	    85	async function runPipeline(flags, io) {
  3001	    86	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
  3002	    87	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  3003	    88	
  3004	    89	  const rawSpec = safeReadJson(io.fs, `${root}/loop.json`);
  3005	    90	  if (!rawSpec.ok) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `no loop.json at ${root} — scaffold one with: baton loop init "<goal>"`);
  3006	    91	  const spec = rawSpec.value;
  3007	    92	  const subtaskError = validateSubtasks(spec?.subtasks);
  3008	    93	  if (subtaskError !== null) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', subtaskError);
  3009	    94	  const { config } = loadConfig(root, io);
  3010	    95	  if (!config) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `no readable baton.config.json at ${root} — the role matrix is required`);
  3011	    96	  const cap = typeof spec?.budgets?.iterationCap === 'number' ? spec.budgets.iterationCap : 5;
  3012	    97	  const timeoutMs = (typeof spec?.budgets?.perRoleTimeoutMin === 'number' ? spec.budgets.perRoleTimeoutMin : 30) * 60_000;
  3013	    98	
  3014	    99	  /** @param {string} role @returns {any | null} */
  3015	   100	  const resolveOne = (role) => {
  3016	   101	    const a = resolveRoles({ config, to: 'claude-code', avoid: [], avoidEntries: [], probes: null }).assignments[role];
  3017	   102	    return a && a.mode !== 'unavailable' ? a : null;
  3018	   103	  };
  3019	   104	
  3020	   105	  // The synthesized phase list makes the loop state machine (phase count,
  3021	   106	  // done detection, journal replay) work unchanged for the subtask cycle.
  3022	   107	  const subtasks = spec.subtasks;
  3023	   108	  const stateSpec = {
  3024	   109	    goal: spec.goal,
  3025	   110	    phases: subtasks.map((/** @type {any} */ st, /** @type {number} */ i) => ({ id: `subtask-${st.id}`, role: `worker-${i % 2 === 0 ? 'a' : 'b'}` })),
  3026	   111	  };
  3027	   112	  /** @type {any} */
  3028	   113	  let state = initLoopState(stateSpec, io);
  3029	   114	  await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
  3030	   115	  const runner = io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild;
  3031	   116	  const seats = worktreePaths(root).seats;
  3032	   117	  let childSeq = 0;
  3033	   118	
  3034	   119	  const transition = async (/** @type {any} */ ev) => {
  3035	   120	    const next = applyLoopEvent(state, ev);
  3036	   121	    const seq = await appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io);
  3037	   122	    state = { ...next, journalSeq: seq };
  3038	   123	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
  3039	   124	    return state;
  3040	   125	  };
  3041	   126	  const park = async (/** @type {string} */ reason) => {
  3042	   127	    await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason });
  3043	   128	    io.stderr.write(`baton pipeline run: parked — ${reason}\n`);
  3044	   129	    return EXIT_PARKED;
  3045	   130	  };
  3046	   131	
  3047	   132	  await setupWorktrees(root, io);
  3048	   133	
  3049	   134	  const spawn = async (/** @type {any} */ assignment, /** @type {string} */ prompt, /** @type {string} */ seatPath, /** @type {string} */ label) => {
  3050	   135	    childSeq += 1;
  3051	   136	    const logPath = `${p.dir}/children/${String(childSeq).padStart(3, '0')}-${label}.log`;
  3052	   137	    ensureDir(io.fs, `${p.dir}/children`);
  3053	   138	    const childSpec = buildChildArgv(assignment, prompt, { root: seatPath });
  3054	   139	    return runner(childSpec, { timeoutMs, graceMs: 10_000, logPath, maxLogBytes: 1_000_000, platform: assignment.platform });
  3055	   140	  };
  3056	   141	
  3057	   142	  for (let i = 0; i < subtasks.length; i += 1) {
  3058	   143	    const st = subtasks[i];
  3059	   144	    const seat = i % 2 === 0 ? 'a' : 'b';
  3060	   145	    const other = seat === 'a' ? 'b' : 'a';
  3061	   146	    const branch = `baton/wt-${seat}/subtask-${st.id}`;
  3062	   147	    const gate = `subtask-${st.id}-review`;
  3063	   148	
  3064	   149	    const writerAssignment = resolveOne(`worker-${seat}`);
  3065	   150	    const reviewerModel = resolveOne(`worker-${other}`);
  3066	   151	    const mergerModel = resolveOne('merger');
  3067	   152	    if (!writerAssignment || !reviewerModel || !mergerModel) {
  3068	   153	      return park(`no eligible assignment for subtask '${st.id}' (worker-${seat}/worker-${other}/merger must all resolve)`);
  3069	   154	    }
  3070	   155	
  3071	   156	    // Start the subtask branch in the writer's seat, then verify the seat.
  3072	   157	    await io.execFile('git', ['checkout', '-b', branch], { cwd: seats[seat] });
  3073	   158	    const pre = await preflightWorktree(root, { seat, branch }, io);
  3074	   159	    if (pre.ok !== true) return park(`preflight refused subtask '${st.id}': ${pre.refusal}`);
  3075	   160	    const mainSha = (await io.execFile('git', ['rev-parse', 'main'], { cwd: root })).stdout.trim();
  3076	   161	
  3077	   162	    let findings = '';
  3078	   163	    let merged = false;
  3079	   164	    while (!merged) {
  3080	   165	      if ((state.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0) >= cap) {
  3081	   166	        await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
  3082	   167	        atomicWriteText(
  3083	   168	          io.fs,
  3084	   169	          `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`,
  3085	   170	          `# Pipeline escalation\n\nGate '${gate}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`,
  3086	   171	        );
  3087	   172	        io.stderr.write(`baton pipeline run: gate '${gate}' hit the ${cap}-iteration cap — escalated (see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md)\n`);
  3088	   173	        return EXIT_ESCALATED;
  3089	   174	      }
  3090	   175	
  3091	   176	      // Writer (write-capable, its own seat).
  3092	   177	      const writerPrompt = [
  3093	   178	        `You are the writer (worker-${seat}) for subtask '${st.id}': ${st.title}.`,
  3094	   179	        `Goal: ${spec.goal}. Work ONLY on branch ${branch} in your worktree; commit your work there.`,
  3095	   180	        findings ? `The previous attempt was BLOCKED — address every finding:\n${findings}` : '',
  3096	   181	      ]
  3097	   182	        .filter(Boolean)
  3098	   183	        .join('\n\n');
  3099	   184	      await spawn({ ...writerAssignment, role: `worker-${seat}` }, writerPrompt, seats[seat], `${st.id}-writer`);
  3100	   185	
  3101	   186	      const post = await postflightWorktree(root, { seat, branch, mainSha }, io);
  3102	   187	      if (post.ok !== true) return park(`postflight refused subtask '${st.id}': ${post.refusal}`);
  3103	   188	
  3104	   189	      // Reviewer (read-only, the OTHER seat's model and cwd, subtask-reviewer role).
  3105	   190	      const reviewPrompt = [
  3106	   191	        `You are the subtask-reviewer for subtask '${st.id}': ${st.title}.`,
  3107	   192	        `Review the diff of ${branch} against main from the ${other} seat (fresh context).`,
  3108	   193	        "End with exactly:\nVERDICT: APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED\nFINDINGS: numbered findings, or 'none'",
  3109	   194	      ].join('\n\n');
  3110	   195	      const review = await spawn({ ...reviewerModel, role: 'subtask-reviewer' }, reviewPrompt, seats[other], `${st.id}-review`);
  3111	   196	      if (review.verdict !== 'APPROVED' && review.verdict !== 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
  3112	   197	        findings = String(review.findings ?? '');
  3113	   198	        const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
  3114	   199	        if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
  3115	   200	          atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`, `# Pipeline escalation\n\nGate '${gate}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`);
  3116	   201	          return EXIT_ESCALATED;
  3117	   202	        }
  3118	   203	        continue;
  3119	   204	      }
  3120	   205	
  3121	   206	      // Merger (read-only child — it re-checks; the SUPERVISOR merges).
  3122	   207	      const mergerPrompt = [
  3123	   208	        `You are the merger for subtask '${st.id}'. Adversarially re-check ${branch} against main before it merges.`,
  3124	   209	        "End with exactly:\nVERDICT: APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED\nFINDINGS: numbered findings, or 'none'",
  3125	   210	      ].join('\n\n');
  3126	   211	      const mergerVerdict = await spawn({ ...mergerModel, role: 'merger' }, mergerPrompt, root, `${st.id}-merge-check`);
  3127	   212	      if (mergerVerdict.verdict !== 'APPROVED' && mergerVerdict.verdict !== 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
  3128	   213	        findings = String(mergerVerdict.findings ?? '');
  3129	   214	        const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
  3130	   215	        if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
  3131	   216	          atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`, `# Pipeline escalation\n\nGate '${gate}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`);
  3132	   217	          return EXIT_ESCALATED;
  3133	   218	        }
  3134	   219	        continue;
  3135	   220	      }
  3136	   221	
  3137	   222	      const merge = await mergeSubtask(root, { branch }, io);
  3138	   223	      if (merge.ok !== true) return park(`merge of '${branch}' refused: ${merge.reason}`);
  3139	   224	      merged = true;
  3140	   225	    }
  3141	   226	
  3142	   227	    await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE });
  3143	   228	  }
  3144	   229	
  3145	   230	  if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { status: state.status, subtasks: subtasks.length } });
  3146	   231	  else io.stdout.write(`baton pipeline run: done — ${subtasks.length} subtask(s) merged\n`);
  3147	   232	  return state.status === LOOP_STATUS.DONE ? 0 : state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED ? EXIT_PARKED : 0;
  3148	   233	}

  3184	    34	/**
  3185	    35	 * `baton loop <subcommand>` — the Layer-2 goal-loop surface. v1 subcommands:
  3186	    36	 * `init "<goal>"` (scaffold loop.json; two-phase, idempotent, dry-run-able).
  3187	    37	 * Supervisor-side by design: the BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD guard deliberately
  3188	    38	 * does NOT apply here.
  3189	    39	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
  3190	    40	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
  3191	    41	 */
  3192	    42	export async function cmdLoop(args, io) {
  3193	    43	  /** @type {string[]} */
  3194	    44	  const positionals = [];
  3195	    45	  /** @type {string[]} */
  3196	    46	  const flagTokens = [];
  3197	    47	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
  3198	    48	    const a = args[i];
  3199	    49	    if (typeof a === 'string' && a.startsWith('--')) {
  3200	    50	      flagTokens.push(a);
  3201	    51	      if (STRING_FLAGS.has(a.slice(2)) && i + 1 < args.length) {
  3202	    52	        i += 1;
  3203	    53	        flagTokens.push(args[i]);
  3204	    54	      }
  3205	    55	    } else {
  3206	    56	      positionals.push(a);
  3207	    57	    }
  3208	    58	  }
  3209	    59	
  3210	    60	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(flagTokens, { 'dry-run': 'boolean', 'approve-smoke': 'string' });
  3211	    61	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'loop', parsed.error);
  3212	    62	  const flags = parsed.flags;
  3213	    63	
  3214	    64	  const sub = positionals[0];
  3215	    65	  if (sub === undefined) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'a subcommand is required — try: baton loop init "<goal>" or baton loop run');
  3216	    66	  if (sub === 'run') {
  3217	    67	    if (positionals.length > 1) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `unexpected argument '${positionals[1]}'`);
  3218	    68	    if (flags['approve-smoke'] === '') return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', '--approve-smoke requires a value (the token from smoke-approval.json)');
  3219	    69	    return runLoop(flags, io);
  3220	    70	  }
  3221	    71	  if (sub !== 'init') return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `unknown subcommand '${sub}' (supported: init, run)`);
  3222	    72	
  3223	    73	  const goal = positionals[1];
  3224	    74	  if (goal === undefined) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'loop init requires a goal — baton loop init "<goal>"');
  3225	    75	  if (positionals.length > 2) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `unexpected argument '${positionals[2]}'`);
  3226	    76	
  3227	    77	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
  3228	    78	  const path = `${root}/loop.json`;
  3229	    79	
  3230	    80	  const finish = (/** @type {any} */ data, /** @type {string} */ human) => {
  3231	    81	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data });
  3232	    82	    else io.stdout.write(`${human}\n`);
  3233	    83	    return 0;
  3234	    84	  };
  3235	    85	
  3236	    86	  // Idempotent: an existing spec is the user's — never overwritten.
  3237	    87	  if (io.fs.existsSync(path)) {
  3238	    88	    return finish({ created: false, path }, `baton loop init: ${path} already exists — left untouched`);
  3239	    89	  }
  3240	    90	  if (flags['dry-run'] === true) {
  3241	    91	    return finish(
  3242	    92	      { created: false, path, dryRun: true },
  3243	    93	      `baton loop init (dry-run): would write ${path} — the default baton/loop@1 spec with goal "${goal}"`,
  3244	    94	    );
  3245	    95	  }
  3246	    96	  ensureDir(io.fs, root);
  3247	    97	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, path, defaultLoopSpec(goal));
  3248	    98	  return finish({ created: true, path }, `baton loop init: wrote ${path} (goal "${goal}") — edit constraints/smoke, then run baton loop run`);
  3249	    99	}
  3250	   100	
  3251	   101	// Exit codes for `loop run` (frozen by the loop-run test contract):
  3252	   102	// 0 done / awaiting-smoke-approval · 1 live-lock refusal · 2 usage error ·
  3253	   103	// 3 escalated · 4 parked.
  3254	   104	const EXIT_ESCALATED = 3;
  3255	   105	const EXIT_PARKED = 4;
  3256	   106	
  3257	   107	/** @param {any} g */
  3258	   108	const gitHeadOf = (g) => g?.headSha ?? 'none';
  3259	   109	/** @param {any} g */
  3260	   110	const gitContentOf = (g) => g?.contentDigest ?? dedupeKey(g ?? null);
  3261	   111	
  3262	   112	/**
  3263	   113	 * The supervisor state machine: drives the spec's phases through headless
  3264	   114	 * role children, records every transition in the journal-replayable loop
  3265	   115	 * state, enforces the 5-cap, pauses at the smoke gate, and hands limit
  3266	   116	 * deaths to the failover transaction. Children run via the injectable seam
  3267	   117	 * `io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild`.
  3268	   118	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags @param {any} io
  3269	   119	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
  3270	   120	 */
  3271	   121	async function runLoop(flags, io) {
  3272	   122	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
  3273	   123	  const specPath = `${root}/loop.json`;
  3274	   124	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  3275	   125	  const lockPath = `${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`;
  3276	   126	
  3277	   127	  // Preconditions — before any lock or spawn.
  3278	   128	  const rawSpec = safeReadJson(io.fs, specPath);
  3279	   129	  if (!rawSpec.ok) {
  3280	   130	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `no loop.json at ${specPath} — scaffold one with: baton loop init "<goal>"`);
  3281	   131	  }
  3282	   132	  const { config } = loadConfig(root, io);
  3283	   133	  if (!config) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `no readable baton.config.json at ${root} — the role matrix is required`);
  3284	   134	  const validated = validateLoopSpec(rawSpec.value, config);
  3285	   135	  if (validated.ok !== true) {
  3286	   136	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `loop.json is invalid:\n  - ${validated.errors.join('\n  - ')}`);
  3287	   137	  }
  3288	   138	  const spec = validated.spec;
  3289	   139	
  3290	   140	  // Supervisor run lock — same provably-dead discipline as the bundle lock:
  3291	   141	  // a live same-host owner is refused; a dead one is reclaimed.
  3292	   142	  const existingLock = safeReadJson(io.fs, lockPath);
  3293	   143	  if (existingLock.ok && existingLock.value && typeof existingLock.value.pid === 'number') {
  3294	   144	    const other = existingLock.value;
  3295	   145	    const alive = typeof io.processAlive === 'function' ? io.processAlive(other.pid) : true;
  3296	   146	    const sameProcess = other.pid === io.pid && other.startTime === io.startTime;
  3297	   147	    if (!sameProcess && (other.host !== io.host || alive)) {
  3298	   148	      io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: another supervisor (pid ${other.pid} on ${other.host}, run ${other.runId}) holds the run lock — refusing a second supervisor\n`);
  3299	   149	      return 1;
  3300	   150	    }
  3301	   151	    io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: reclaimed the run lock from a provably-dead supervisor (pid ${other.pid})\n`);
  3302	   152	  }
  3303	   153	
  3304	   154	  // Load or initialize the run state.
  3305	   155	  let { state } = await loadLoopState(root, io);
  3306	   156	  if (state === null) {
  3307	   157	    state = initLoopState(spec, io);
  3308	   158	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
  3309	   159	  }
  3310	   160	  const runId = state.runId;
  3311	   161	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  3312	   162	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, lockPath, { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, runId });
  3313	   163	
  3314	   164	  /** Apply one event, journal it, persist the snapshot — one transition. */
  3315	   165	  const transition = async (/** @type {any} */ ev) => {
  3316	   166	    const next = applyLoopEvent(state, ev);
  3317	   167	    const seq = await appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io);
  3318	   168	    state = { ...next, journalSeq: seq };
  3319	   169	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
  3320	   170	    return state;
  3321	   171	  };
  3322	   172	
  3323	   173	  const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
  3324	   174	  const runner = io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild;
  3325	   175	  const sessionHint = `loop-${runId}`;
  3326	   176	  /** @type {string[]} */
  3327	   177	  let avoid = [];
  3328	   178	  /** @type {Array<{platform: string, model: string}>} */
  3329	   179	  let avoidEntries = [];
  3330	   180	  let childSeq = 0;
  3331	   181	
  3332	   182	  try {
  3333	   183	    // --approve-smoke: verify the presented token against RECOMPUTED inputs;
  3334	   184	    // drift parks (the human approved a state that no longer exists).
  3335	   185	    if (typeof flags['approve-smoke'] === 'string') {
  3336	   186	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) {
  3337	   187	        return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `--approve-smoke only applies while the run awaits smoke approval (status: ${state.status})`);
  3338	   188	      }
  3339	   189	      const record = safeReadJson(io.fs, `${p.dir}/smoke-approval.json`);
  3340	   190	      if (!record.ok) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'no smoke-approval.json found — run the loop to the smoke gate first');
  3341	   191	      const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
  3342	   192	      const currentInputs = {
  3343	   193	        stateDigest: dedupeKey(io.fs.readFileSync(p.state, 'utf8')),
  3344	   194	        smokeCmd: String(spec.smoke?.cmd ?? ''),
  3345	   195	        smokeOutputDigest: String(record.value?.inputs?.smokeOutputDigest ?? ''),
  3346	   196	        gitHead: gitHeadOf(git),
  3347	   197	        gitContentDigest: gitContentOf(git),
  3348	   198	        childAssignment: String(record.value?.inputs?.childAssignment ?? ''),
  3349	   199	      };
  3350	   200	      const verify = verifySmokeToken(String(flags['approve-smoke']), currentInputs);
  3351	   201	      if (verify.ok !== true) {
  3352	   202	        const drifted = 'driftedInputs' in verify ? verify.driftedInputs.join(', ') : 'unknown';
  3353	   203	        await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: `smoke approval token is stale — drifted inputs: ${drifted}; re-run the smoke gate` });
  3354	   204	        io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: smoke approval REFUSED — inputs drifted since the token was issued (${drifted}); the run is parked for a fresh smoke pass\n`);
  3355	   205	        return EXIT_PARKED;
  3356	   206	      }
  3357	   207	      await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE, token: flags['approve-smoke'], verified: true });
  3358	   208	      io.stdout.write('baton loop run: smoke approval verified — continuing the run\n');
  3359	   209	    }
  3360	   210	
  3361	   211	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
  3362	   212	      io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: the run is escalated (gate ${state.escalation?.gate}) — see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md\n`);
  3363	   213	      return EXIT_ESCALATED;
  3364	   214	    }
  3365	   215	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) {
  3366	   216	      io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: the run is parked — ${state.parkReason ?? 'no reason recorded'}\n`);
  3367	   217	      return EXIT_PARKED;
  3368	   218	    }
  3369	   219	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) {
  3370	   220	      io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: awaiting smoke approval — resume with: baton loop run --approve-smoke <token from ${p.dir}/smoke-approval.json>\n`);
  3371	   221	      return 0;
  3372	   222	    }
  3373	   223	
  3374	   224	    // ---- The phase loop. ---------------------------------------------------
  3375	   225	    while (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING && state.phaseIndex < state.phaseCount) {
  3376	   226	      const phase = spec.phases[state.phaseIndex];
  3377	   227	      const cap = spec.budgets.iterationCap;
  3378	   228	      let findings = '';
  3379	   229	      let failoverAttempt = 0;
  3380	   230	      /** @type {any | null} */
  3381	   231	      let forcedAssignment = null;
  3382	   232	      /** @type {string | null} */
  3383	   233	      let forcedPrompt = null;
  3384	   234	      let phaseDone = false;
  3385	   235	
  3386	   236	      while (!phaseDone) {
  3387	   237	        // The cap gates the SPAWN: at the cap the reducer escalates and a
  3388	   238	        // 6th child never starts.
  3389	   239	        if ((state.iterations?.[phase.id] ?? 0) >= cap) {
  3390	   240	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: phase.id, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
  3391	   241	          atomicWriteText(
  3392	   242	            io.fs,
  3393	   243	            `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`,
  3394	   244	            `# Loop escalation\n\nGate '${phase.id}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap on run ${runId}.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n\nResolve the findings, then resume with baton loop run.\n`,
  3395	   245	          );
  3396	   246	          io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: gate '${phase.id}' hit the ${cap}-iteration cap — escalated (see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md)\n`);
  3397	   247	          return EXIT_ESCALATED;
  3398	   248	        }
  3399	   249	        if (childSeq >= spec.budgets.maxChildrenPerPhase * spec.phases.length) {
  3400	   250	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: 'total child budget exhausted' });
  3401	   251	          return EXIT_PARKED;
  3402	   252	        }
  3403	   253	
  3404	   254	        const resolved =
  3405	   255	          forcedAssignment ??
  3406	   256	          (() => {
  3407	   257	            const r = resolveRoles({ config, to: 'claude-code', avoid, avoidEntries, probes: null }).assignments[phase.role];
  3408	   258	            return r && r.mode !== 'unavailable' ? r : null;
  3409	   259	          })();
  3410	   260	        if (!resolved) {
  3411	   261	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: `no eligible platform/model for role '${phase.role}'` });
  3412	   262	          io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: no eligible assignment for role '${phase.role}' — parked\n`);
  3413	   263	          return EXIT_PARKED;
  3414	   264	        }
  3415	   265	        const assignment = { ...resolved, role: phase.role };
  3416	   266	        const prompt =
  3417	   267	          forcedPrompt ??
  3418	   268	          [
  3419	   269	            `You are the ${phase.role} for loop run ${runId}, phase '${phase.id}'.`,
  3420	   270	            `Goal: ${spec.goal}`,
  3421	   271	            spec.constraints.length > 0 ? `Constraints:\n${spec.constraints.map((/** @type {string} */ c) => `- ${c}`).join('\n')}` : '',
  3422	   272	            findings ? `The previous attempt was BLOCKED with these findings — address every one:\n${findings}` : '',
  3423	   273	            "End with exactly:\nVERDICT: APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED\nFINDINGS: numbered findings, or 'none'",
  3424	   274	          ]
  3425	   275	            .filter(Boolean)
  3426	   276	            .join('\n\n');
  3427	   277	        forcedAssignment = null;
  3428	   278	        forcedPrompt = null;
  3429	   279	
  3430	   280	        childSeq += 1;
  3431	   281	        const logPath = `${p.dir}/children/${String(childSeq).padStart(3, '0')}-${phase.id}.log`;
  3432	   282	        ensureDir(io.fs, `${p.dir}/children`);
  3433	   283	        const childSpec = buildChildArgv(assignment, prompt, { root });
  3434	   284	        const result = await runner(childSpec, {
  3435	   285	          timeoutMs: spec.budgets.perRoleTimeoutMin * 60_000,
  3436	   286	          graceMs: 10_000,
  3437	   287	          logPath,
  3438	   288	          maxLogBytes: 1_000_000,
  3439	   289	          platform: assignment.platform,
  3440	   290	        });
  3441	   291	
  3442	   292	        // Classification reads the child's LOG (the frozen transcript), so a
  3443	   293	        // limit banner routes to failover even when a verdict parsed.
  3444	   294	        const transcript = io.fs.existsSync(logPath) ? io.fs.readFileSync(logPath, 'utf8') : '';
  3445	   295	        const cls = classify({ text: transcript, exitCode: result.exitCode ?? 0, platform: assignment.platform, table }).class;
  3446	   296	
  3447	   297	        if (cls === 'usage-limit' || cls === 'model-unavailable' || cls === 'other-error' || cls === 'throttle' || cls === 'auth') {
  3448	   298	          failoverAttempt += 1;
  3449	   299	          const decision = await runFailover({
  3450	   300	            root,
  3451	   301	            io,
  3452	   302	            config,
  3453	   303	            role: phase.role,
  3454	   304	            assignment,
  3455	   305	            transcript,
  3456	   306	            exitCode: result.exitCode ?? 1,
  3457	   307	            sessionHint,
  3458	   308	            probes: null,
  3459	   309	            avoid,
  3460	   310	            avoidEntries,
  3461	   311	            attempt: failoverAttempt,
  3462	   312	            loopState: { runId, phaseIndex: state.phaseIndex, iterations: state.iterations, status: state.status },
  3463	   313	          });
  3464	   314	          if (decision.action === 'park') {
  3465	   315	            await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: decision.reason });
  3466	   316	            io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: parked — ${decision.reason}${'resumeAt' in decision && decision.resumeAt ? ` (resume around ${decision.resumeAt})` : ''}\n`);
  3467	   317	            return EXIT_PARKED;
  3468	   318	          }
  3469	   319	          if (decision.action === 'relaunch') {
  3470	   320	            // Platform avoidance is PER-DEATH, not cumulative: runFailover
  3471	   321	            // already avoided the just-died platform when it resolved the
  3472	   322	            // relaunch. Accumulating here would exhaust every platform on a
  3473	   323	            // chained A→B→A failover and park instead of returning to A
  3474	   324	            // (acceptance constraint 2). Entry-level avoidance (dead MODELS)
  3475	   325	            // does accumulate — a rejected model stays rejected.
  3476	   326	            if (Array.isArray(decision.avoidEntries)) avoidEntries = decision.avoidEntries;
  3477	   327	            forcedAssignment = { ...decision.assignment, role: phase.role };
  3478	   328	            forcedPrompt = typeof decision.prompt === 'string' && decision.prompt.length > 0 ? decision.prompt : null;
  3479	   329	            continue; // relaunch the same phase on the new assignment
  3480	   330	          }
  3481	   331	          continue; // retry: re-spawn the same phase once more
  3482	   332	        }
  3483	   333	
  3484	   334	        const verdict = String(result.verdict ?? 'BLOCKED');
  3485	   335	        if (verdict === 'APPROVED' || verdict === 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
  3486	   336	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE });
  3487	   337	          phaseDone = true;
  3488	   338	
  3489	   339	          // The smoke gate fires right after the smoke-build phase completes.
  3490	   340	          if (phase.id === 'smoke-build' && typeof spec.smoke?.cmd === 'string' && spec.smoke.cmd.length > 0) {
  3491	   341	            const [cmd, ...cmdArgs] = spec.smoke.cmd.split(/\s+/);
  3492	   342	            let smokeOut = '';
  3493	   343	            try {
  3494	   344	              const r = await io.execFile(cmd, cmdArgs, { cwd: root });
  3495	   345	              smokeOut = `${r?.stdout ?? ''}${r?.stderr ?? ''}`;
  3496	   346	            } catch (err) {
  3497	   347	              smokeOut = `smoke command failed: ${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err)}`;
  3498	   348	            }
  3499	   349	            await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT });
  3500	   350	            const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
  3501	   351	            const inputs = {
  3502	   352	              stateDigest: dedupeKey(io.fs.readFileSync(p.state, 'utf8')),
  3503	   353	              smokeCmd: spec.smoke.cmd,
  3504	   354	              smokeOutputDigest: dedupeKey(smokeOut),
  3505	   355	              gitHead: gitHeadOf(git),
  3506	   356	              gitContentDigest: gitContentOf(git),
  3507	   357	              childAssignment: JSON.stringify({ platform: assignment.platform, model: assignment.model }),
  3508	   358	            };
  3509	   359	            const token = smokeApprovalToken(inputs);
  3510	   360	            atomicWriteJson(io.fs, `${p.dir}/smoke-approval.json`, { token, inputs, issuedAt: io.now() });
  3511	   361	            atomicWriteText(
  3512	   362	              io.fs,
  3513	   363	              `${p.dir}/SMOKE-REVIEW.md`,
  3514	   364	              `# Smoke gate — human approval required\n\nRun: ${runId}\nCommand: \`${spec.smoke.cmd}\`\nExpected: ${spec.smoke.expect ?? '(unspecified)'}\n\n## Output\n\n\`\`\`\n${smokeOut}\n\`\`\`\n\nApprove with:\n\n    baton loop run --approve-smoke ${token}\n\nThe token is bound to the current state/output/git — any drift refuses it.\n`,
  3515	   365	            );
  3516	   366	            io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: smoke gate reached — review ${p.dir}/SMOKE-REVIEW.md and approve with --approve-smoke <token>\n`);
  3517	   367	            return 0;
  3518	   368	          }
  3519	   369	        } else {
  3520	   370	          // BLOCKED (or unparseable, already coerced to BLOCKED upstream).
  3521	   371	          findings = String(result.findings ?? '');
  3522	   372	          const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: phase.id, verdict });
  3523	   373	          if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
  3524	   374	            atomicWriteText(
  3525	   375	              io.fs,
  3526	   376	              `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`,
  3527	   377	              `# Loop escalation\n\nGate '${phase.id}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap on run ${runId}.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`,
  3528	   378	            );
  3529	   379	            io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: gate '${phase.id}' escalated at the cap — see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md\n`);
  3530	   380	            return EXIT_ESCALATED;
  3531	   381	          }
  3532	   382	        }
  3533	   383	      }
  3534	   384	    }
  3535	   385	
  3536	   386	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.DONE || state.phaseIndex >= state.phaseCount) {
  3537	   387	      io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: done — every phase completed (run ${runId})\n`);
  3538	   388	      return 0;
  3539	   389	    }
  3540	   390	    io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: stopped in status ${state.status}\n`);
  3541	   391	    return state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED ? EXIT_PARKED : state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED ? EXIT_ESCALATED : 0;
  3542	   392	  } finally {
  3543	   393	    // The lock is per-invocation: release on every exit path so a paused run
  3544	   394	    // (awaiting approval) can be resumed by the next invocation.
  3545	   395	    try {
  3546	   396	      if (io.fs.existsSync(lockPath)) io.fs.unlinkSync(lockPath);
  3547	   397	    } catch {
  3548	   398	      // Best effort — a stale lock is recoverable via the provably-dead path.
  3549	   399	    }
  3550	   400	  }
  3551	   401	}

  3561	     1	/**
  3562	     2	 * Loop run state — snapshot + journal + replay for the Layer-2 supervisor,

  3569	     9	import { appendEntry, readAllTolerant } from '../util/jsonl.mjs';
  3570	    10	import { atomicWriteJson, safeReadJson, ensureDir } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
  3571	    11	import { dedupeKey } from '../util/ids.mjs';
  3572	    12	
  3573	    13	export const LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA = 'baton/loop-state@1';
  3574	    14	
  3575	    15	/** The frozen status vocabulary. */
  3576	    16	export const LOOP_STATUS = Object.freeze({
  3577	    17	  RUNNING: 'running',
  3578	    18	  AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL: 'awaiting-smoke-approval',
  3579	    19	  PARKED: 'parked',
  3580	    20	  ESCALATED: 'escalated',
  3581	    21	  DONE: 'done',
  3582	    22	});
  3583	    23	
  3584	    24	/** The frozen journal event vocabulary. */
  3585	    25	export const LOOP_EVENT = Object.freeze({
  3586	    26	  PHASE_ADVANCE: 'phase-advance',
  3587	    27	  GATE_ITERATION: 'gate-iteration',
  3588	    28	  PARK: 'park',
  3589	    29	  RESUME: 'resume',
  3590	    30	  SMOKE_AWAIT: 'smoke-await',
  3591	    31	  SMOKE_APPROVE: 'smoke-approve',
  3592	    32	});
  3593	    33	
  3594	    34	// Every gate loops at most 5 times — a repo-wide hard invariant (AGENTS.md
  3595	    35	// §Review gates); the reducer refuses a 6th and escalates instead.
  3596	    36	const GATE_ITERATION_CAP = 5;
  3597	    37	
  3598	    38	/** @param {string} root */
  3599	    39	export function loopPaths(root) {
  3600	    40	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff/loop`;
  3601	    41	  return { dir, state: `${dir}/state.json`, journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson` };
  3602	    42	}
  3603	    43	
  3604	    44	/**
  3605	    45	 * Fresh run state — pure and deterministic from the injected io (identity
  3606	    46	 * facts + clock only; no Date.now/Math.random, which would break replay).
  3607	    47	 * @param {any} spec a VALIDATED loop spec @param {any} io
  3608	    48	 */
  3609	    49	export function initLoopState(spec, io) {
  3610	    50	  const runId = `loop-${dedupeKey({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, at: io.now() }).slice(0, 12)}`;
  3611	    51	  /** @type {Record<string, number>} */
  3612	    52	  const iterations = {};
  3613	    53	  for (const p of spec.phases ?? []) {
  3614	    54	    if (/gate/.test(String(p.id))) iterations[p.id] = 0;
  3615	    55	  }
  3616	    56	  return {
  3617	    57	    schema: LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA,
  3618	    58	    runId,
  3619	    59	    goal: spec.goal,
  3620	    60	    phaseCount: Array.isArray(spec.phases) ? spec.phases.length : 0,
  3621	    61	    phaseIndex: 0,
  3622	    62	    iterations,
  3623	    63	    status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING,
  3624	    64	    parkReason: null,
  3625	    65	    escalation: null,
  3626	    66	    smokeApproval: null,
  3627	    67	    createdAt: io.now(),
  3628	    68	    journalSeq: 0,
  3629	    69	  };
  3630	    70	}
  3631	    71	
  3632	    72	/**
  3633	    73	 * The pure transition reducer. Never mutates its input; unknown events and
  3634	    74	 * refused transitions return the input state unchanged (a new reference is
  3635	    75	 * only produced for a real transition).
  3636	    76	 * @param {any} state @param {any} ev
  3637	    77	 */
  3638	    78	export function applyLoopEvent(state, ev) {
  3639	    79	  switch (ev?.type) {
  3640	    80	    case LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE: {
  3641	    81	      const next = state.phaseIndex + 1;
  3642	    82	      if (next >= state.phaseCount) return { ...state, phaseIndex: state.phaseCount, status: LOOP_STATUS.DONE };
  3643	    83	      return { ...state, phaseIndex: next };
  3644	    84	    }
  3645	    85	    case LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION: {
  3646	    86	      const gate = String(ev.gate);
  3647	    87	      const current = state.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0;
  3648	    88	      // The 6th attempt is REFUSED: the counter stays at the cap and the run
  3649	    89	      // escalates with a record naming the gate — never a 6th run.
  3650	    90	      if (current >= GATE_ITERATION_CAP) {
  3651	    91	        return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, escalation: { gate, iteration: current } };
  3652	    92	      }
  3653	    93	      return { ...state, iterations: { ...state.iterations, [gate]: current + 1 } };
  3654	    94	    }
  3655	    95	    case LOOP_EVENT.PARK:
  3656	    96	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, parkReason: ev.reason ?? null };
  3657	    97	    case LOOP_EVENT.RESUME:
  3658	    98	      // Only a PARK is resumable; an escalation is an operator decision.
  3659	    99	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) return state;
  3660	   100	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, parkReason: null };
  3661	   101	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT:
  3662	   102	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL };
  3663	   103	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE: {
  3664	   104	      // Guarded transition: only meaningful while awaiting, and only with the
  3665	   105	      // caller's verifySmokeToken result stamped verified === true. A missing
  3666	   106	      // or false `verified` is unverified — it never resumes the run.
  3667	   107	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) return state;
  3668	   108	      if (ev.verified !== true) return state;
  3669	   109	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, smokeApproval: { token: ev.token, at: ev.ts ?? null } };
  3670	   110	    }
  3671	   111	    default:
  3672	   112	      return state;
  3673	   113	  }
  3674	   114	}
  3675	   115	
  3676	   116	/**
  3677	   117	 * Atomic snapshot write (tmp + rename under .handoff/loop).
  3678	   118	 * @param {string} root @param {any} state @param {any} io
  3679	   119	 */
  3680	   120	export async function writeLoopState(root, state, io) {
  3681	   121	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  3682	   122	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  3683	   123	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, p.state, state);
  3684	   124	}
  3685	   125	
  3686	   126	/**
  3687	   127	 * Append one journal event; allocates the next seq past
  3688	   128	 * max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) unless the entry carries one.
  3689	   129	 * @param {string} root @param {any} ev @param {any} io
  3690	   130	 * @returns {Promise<number>} the persisted seq
  3691	   131	 */
  3692	   132	export async function appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io) {
  3693	   133	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  3694	   134	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  3695	   135	  let seq = ev.seq;
  3696	   136	  if (typeof seq !== 'number') {
  3697	   137	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.state);
  3698	   138	    const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
  3699	   139	    let tailSeq = 0;
  3700	   140	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  3701	   141	      for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
  3702	   142	        if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
  3703	   143	      }
  3704	   144	    }
  3705	   145	    seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;
  3706	   146	  }
  3707	   147	  const ts = typeof ev.ts === 'string' ? ev.ts : io.now();
  3708	   148	  const entry = {
  3709	   149	    ...ev,
  3710	   150	    seq,
  3711	   151	    ts,
  3712	   152	    dedupeKey: typeof ev.dedupeKey === 'string' && ev.dedupeKey.length > 0 ? ev.dedupeKey : dedupeKey({ seq, ts, type: ev.type, gate: ev.gate ?? null }),
  3713	   153	  };
  3714	   154	  appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, entry);
  3715	   155	  return seq;
  3716	   156	}
  3717	   157	
  3718	   158	/**
  3719	   159	 * Load = snapshot + torn-tail-tolerant replay of journal events PAST the
  3720	   160	 * snapshot's seq. Duplicate seqs/dedupeKeys apply exactly once, so a
  3721	   161	 * supervisor-death recovery never re-runs a completed iteration.
  3722	   162	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
  3723	   163	 * @returns {Promise<{state: any, warnings: string[]}>}
  3724	   164	 */
  3725	   165	export async function loadLoopState(root, io) {
  3726	   166	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  3727	   167	  /** @type {string[]} */
  3728	   168	  const warnings = [];
  3729	   169	  const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.state);
  3730	   170	  if (!snap.ok) return { state: null, warnings: ['no loop state snapshot found'] };
  3731	   171	  let state = snap.value;
  3732	   172	  const baseSeq = typeof state.journalSeq === 'number' ? state.journalSeq : 0;
  3733	   173	
  3734	   174	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  3735	   175	    const { entries, warnings: jw } = readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal);
  3736	   176	    for (const w of jw ?? []) warnings.push(`journal: ${w.kind ?? 'warning'} at line ${w.line ?? '?'}`);
  3737	   177	    const applied = new Set();
  3738	   178	    let maxSeq = baseSeq;
  3739	   179	    for (const e of entries) {
  3740	   180	      const seq = typeof e.seq === 'number' ? e.seq : null;
  3741	   181	      if (seq !== null && seq <= baseSeq) continue; // already folded into the snapshot
  3742	   182	      const key = e.dedupeKey ?? (seq !== null ? `seq:${seq}` : null);
  3743	   183	      if (key !== null && applied.has(key)) continue; // replayed duplicate — apply once
  3744	   184	      if (seq !== null && applied.has(`seq:${seq}`)) continue;
  3745	   185	      state = applyLoopEvent(state, e);
  3746	   186	      if (key !== null) applied.add(key);
  3747	   187	      if (seq !== null) {
  3748	   188	        applied.add(`seq:${seq}`);
  3749	   189	        if (seq > maxSeq) maxSeq = seq;
  3750	   190	      }
  3751	   191	    }
  3752	   192	    state = { ...state, journalSeq: maxSeq };
  3753	   193	  }
  3754	   194	  return { state, warnings };
  3755	   195	}
  3756	   196	
  3757	   197	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3758	   198	// Smoke-approval token — a STRUCTURED digest over the six bound inputs, so a
  3759	   199	// stale approval is refused NAMING what moved (mirrors the receive receipt
  3760	   200	// token's driftedInputs discipline).
  3761	   201	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3762	   202	
  3763	   203	const SMOKE_TOKEN_PREFIX = 'smk1';
  3764	   204	const SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS = /** @type {const} */ ([
  3765	   205	  'stateDigest',
  3766	   206	  'smokeCmd',
  3767	   207	  'smokeOutputDigest',
  3768	   208	  'gitHead',
  3769	   209	  'gitContentDigest',
  3770	   210	  'childAssignment',
  3771	   211	]);
  3772	   212	
  3773	   213	/**
  3774	   214	 * Deterministic structured token. Per-field digests keep field boundaries
  3775	   215	 * (no concatenation collisions) and let verification name drifted inputs.
  3776	   216	 * Missing bound fields throw — a token over partial inputs is meaningless.
  3777	   217	 * @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
  3778	   218	 * @returns {string}
  3779	   219	 */
  3780	   220	export function smokeApprovalToken(inputs) {
  3781	   221	  for (const f of SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS) {
  3782	   222	    if (typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') throw new Error(`smoke approval token: required input '${f}' is missing`);
  3783	   223	  }
  3784	   224	  return `${SMOKE_TOKEN_PREFIX}.` + SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.map((f) => dedupeKey(inputs[f]).slice(0, 10)).join('.');
  3785	   225	}
  3786	   226	
  3787	   227	/**
  3788	   228	 * Recompute against current inputs; positional comparison names every
  3789	   229	 * drifted input. Never throws — partial current inputs simply cannot verify.
  3790	   230	 * @param {string} presented @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
  3791	   231	 * @returns {{ok: true} | {ok: false, driftedInputs: string[]}}
  3792	   232	 */
  3793	   233	export function verifySmokeToken(presented, inputs) {
  3794	   234	  /** @type {string} */
  3795	   235	  let current;
  3796	   236	  try {
  3797	   237	    current = smokeApprovalToken(inputs);
  3798	   238	  } catch {
  3799	   239	    return { ok: false, driftedInputs: SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.filter((f) => typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') };
  3800	   240	  }
  3801	   241	  if (presented === current) return { ok: true };
  3802	   242	  const a = String(presented ?? '').split('.');
  3803	   243	  const b = current.split('.');
  3804	   244	  /** @type {string[]} */
  3805	   245	  const drifted = [];
  3806	   246	  SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.forEach((f, i) => {
  3807	   247	    if (a[i + 1] !== b[i + 1]) drifted.push(f);
  3808	   248	  });
  3809	   249	  return { ok: false, driftedInputs: drifted.length > 0 ? drifted : [...SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS] };
  3810	   250	}

  3815	     3	 * checked transaction: namespaced branches only, pre/postflight assertions
  3816	     4	 * around each child, merges under a supervisor merge lock gated on a

  3818	     6	 * branch jail so nothing outside baton/wt-* is ever touched. Plan:
  3819	     7	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"Worktree transaction
  3820	     8	 * layer" + §"Attribution enforcement for child commits".

  3822	    10	import { ensureIgnoreLine } from '../scaffold/gitignore.mjs';

  3842	    30	/** @param {string} root */
  3843	    31	export function worktreePaths(root) {
  3844	    32	  const dir = `${root}/.worktrees`;
  3845	    33	  /** @type {Record<string, string>} */
  3846	    34	  const seats = { a: `${dir}/wt-a`, b: `${dir}/wt-b` };
  3847	    35	  return { dir, seats };

  3849	    37	
  3850	    38	/** @param {string} seat */
  3851	    39	const seatBranch = (seat) => `baton/wt-${seat}/base`;
  3852	    40	
  3853	    41	/** @param {any} io @param {string} cwd @param {string[]} args */
  3854	    42	async function git(io, cwd, args) {
  3855	    43	  return io.execFile('git', args, { cwd });
  3856	    44	}
  3857	    45	
  3858	    46	/** Parse `git worktree list --porcelain` into [{path, branch}]. @param {string} out */
  3859	    47	function parseWorktreeList(out) {
  3860	    48	  /** @type {Array<{path: string, branch: string | null}>} */
  3861	    49	  const entries = [];
  3862	    50	  for (const block of String(out).split(/\n\n+/)) {
  3863	    51	    const path = block.match(/^worktree (.+)$/m)?.[1];
  3864	    52	    if (!path) continue;
  3865	    53	    const branch = block.match(/^branch refs\/heads\/(.+)$/m)?.[1] ?? null;
  3866	    54	    entries.push({ path, branch });
  3867	    55	  }
  3868	    56	  return entries;
  3869	    57	}
  3870	    58	
  3871	    59	/**
  3872	    60	 * Ensure both seat worktrees exist on their namespaced branches and that
  3873	    61	 * `.worktrees/` is gitignored. Idempotent: an existing seat is left alone.
  3874	    62	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
  3875	    63	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, worktrees: Array<{seat: string, path: string, branch: string}>, gitignoreChanged: boolean}>}
  3876	    64	 */
  3877	    65	export async function setupWorktrees(root, io) {
  3878	    66	  const p = worktreePaths(root);
  3879	    67	
  3880	    68	  const gitignorePath = `${root}/.gitignore`;
  3881	    69	  const existing = io.fs.existsSync(gitignorePath) ? io.fs.readFileSync(gitignorePath, 'utf8') : '';
  3882	    70	  const ensured = ensureIgnoreLine(existing, '.worktrees/');
  3883	    71	  if (ensured.changed) io.fs.writeFileSync(gitignorePath, ensured.text);
  3884	    72	
  3885	    73	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
  3886	    74	  /** @type {Array<{seat: string, path: string, branch: string}>} */
  3887	    75	  const worktrees = [];
  3888	    76	  for (const seat of SEATS) {
  3889	    77	    const path = p.seats[seat];
  3890	    78	    const present = listed.find((e) => e.path === path);
  3891	    79	    if (present) {
  3892	    80	      worktrees.push({ seat, path, branch: present.branch ?? seatBranch(seat) });
  3893	    81	      continue;
  3894	    82	    }
  3895	    83	    const branch = seatBranch(seat);
  3896	    84	    await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'add', '-b', branch, path]);
  3897	    85	    worktrees.push({ seat, path, branch });
  3898	    86	  }
  3899	    87	  return { ok: true, worktrees, gitignoreChanged: ensured.changed };
  3900	    88	}
  3901	    89	
  3902	    90	/**
  3903	    91	 * Preflight before a child runs in a seat: the worktree is listed, its index
  3904	    92	 * is clean, and HEAD sits on the expected branch. Refusals name the failed
  3905	    93	 * check and never issue destructive git.
  3906	    94	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string}} target @param {any} io
  3907	    95	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, check: string, refusal: string}>}
  3908	    96	 */
  3909	    97	export async function preflightWorktree(root, target, io) {
  3910	    98	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
  3911	    99	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
  3912	   100	  if (!listed.some((e) => e.path === path)) {
  3913	   101	    return { ok: false, check: 'missing-worktree', refusal: `worktree ${path} is not listed by git — run setup or self-heal first` };
  3914	   102	  }
  3915	   103	  const status = (await git(io, path, ['status', '--porcelain'])).stdout.trim();
  3916	   104	  if (status.length > 0) {
  3917	   105	    return { ok: false, check: 'dirty-index', refusal: `worktree ${path} has a dirty index/working tree — a clean seat is required before a child runs` };
  3918	   106	  }
  3919	   107	  const head = (await git(io, path, ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'])).stdout.trim();
  3920	   108	  if (head !== target.branch) {
  3921	   109	    return { ok: false, check: 'wrong-branch', refusal: `worktree ${path} HEAD is on '${head}', expected branch '${target.branch}'` };
  3922	   110	  }
  3923	   111	  return { ok: true };
  3924	   112	}
  3925	   113	
  3926	   114	/**
  3927	   115	 * Postflight after a child: HEAD still on the expected branch (checked in the
  3928	   116	 * seat cwd) and main unmoved from the pre-child capture.
  3929	   117	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string, mainSha: string}} target @param {any} io
  3930	   118	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, check: string, refusal: string}>}
  3931	   119	 */
  3932	   120	export async function postflightWorktree(root, target, io) {
  3933	   121	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
  3934	   122	  const head = (await git(io, path, ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'])).stdout.trim();
  3935	   123	  if (head !== target.branch) {
  3936	   124	    return { ok: false, check: 'head-drift', refusal: `the child left HEAD on '${head}' instead of the expected branch '${target.branch}'` };
  3937	   125	  }
  3938	   126	  const main = (await git(io, root, ['rev-parse', 'main'])).stdout.trim();
  3939	   127	  if (main !== target.mainSha) {
  3940	   128	    return { ok: false, check: 'main-moved', refusal: `main moved under the child (was ${target.mainSha}, now ${main}) — only the merger writes main` };
  3941	   129	  }
  3942	   130	  return { ok: true };
  3943	   131	}
  3944	   132	
  3945	   133	/**
  3946	   134	 * Full-range attribution scan over main..branch — the merge gate. Every
  3947	   135	 * commit must be authored AND committed by the sole author, with no forbidden
  3948	   136	 * AI-attribution trailer in the body. The range is never windowed.
  3949	   137	 * @param {string} root @param {string} branch @param {any} io
  3950	   138	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, offending: string, reason: string}>}
  3951	   139	 */
  3952	   140	export async function attributionScan(root, branch, io) {
  3953	   141	  const out = (await git(io, root, ['log', `main..${branch}`, `--format=${LOG_FORMAT}`])).stdout;
  3954	   142	  for (const record of String(out).split('\x1e')) {
  3955	   143	    if (record.trim().length === 0) continue;
  3956	   144	    const [sha, an, ae, cn, ce, body = ''] = record.replace(/^\n/, '').split('\x00');
  3957	   145	    if (an !== SOLE_AUTHOR.name || ae !== SOLE_AUTHOR.email) {
  3958	   146	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} has a foreign author identity (${an} <${ae}>) — sole-author is required` };
  3959	   147	    }
  3960	   148	    if (cn !== SOLE_AUTHOR.name || ce !== SOLE_AUTHOR.email) {
  3961	   149	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} has a foreign committer identity (${cn} <${ce}>) — sole-author is required` };
  3962	   150	    }
  3963	   151	    if (TRAILER_PATTERNS.some((re) => re.test(body))) {
  3964	   152	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} carries a forbidden AI-attribution trailer (co-authored/generated-with/emoji) in its message` };
  3965	   153	    }
  3966	   154	  }
  3967	   155	  return { ok: true };
  3968	   156	}
  3969	   157	
  3970	   158	const mergeLockPath = (/** @type {string} */ root) => `${root}/.handoff/loop/merge.lock`;
  3971	   159	
  3972	   160	/**
  3973	   161	 * Merge an approved subtask branch into main — one guarded transaction:
  3974	   162	 * acquire the merge lock (a held lock refuses), gate on the attribution scan,
  3975	   163	 * merge at the repo root, abort-and-park on conflict, then ff-only sync both
  3976	   164	 * seats. The lock is released on EVERY exit path after acquisition.
  3977	   165	 * @param {string} root @param {{branch: string}} input @param {any} io
  3978	   166	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true, branch: string} | {ok: false, reason: string}>}
  3979	   167	 */
  3980	   168	export async function mergeSubtask(root, input, io) {
  3981	   169	  const lock = mergeLockPath(root);
  3982	   170	  if (io.fs.existsSync(lock)) {
  3983	   171	    return { ok: false, reason: `the merge lock at ${lock} is held by another merger — refusing a concurrent merge` };
  3984	   172	  }
  3985	   173	  ensureDir(io.fs, `${root}/.handoff/loop`);
  3986	   174	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, lock, { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, at: io.now() });
  3987	   175	  try {
  3988	   176	    const scan = await attributionScan(root, input.branch, io);
  3989	   177	    if (scan.ok !== true) return { ok: false, reason: scan.reason };
  3990	   178	
  3991	   179	    try {
  3992	   180	      await git(io, root, ['merge', input.branch]);
  3993	   181	    } catch (err) {
  3994	   182	      // Conflict (or any merge failure): abort, never auto-resolve — park.
  3995	   183	      try {
  3996	   184	        await git(io, root, ['merge', '--abort']);
  3997	   185	      } catch {
  3998	   186	        // Nothing to abort — the failure predates a merge state.
  3999	   187	      }
  4000	   188	      const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.stdout || /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message || String(err);
  4001	   189	      return { ok: false, reason: `merge conflict — aborted and parked for the operator: ${String(msg).split('\n')[0]}` };
  4002	   190	    }
  4003	   191	
  4004	   192	    const seats = worktreePaths(root).seats;
  4005	   193	    for (const seat of SEATS) {
  4006	   194	      try {
  4007	   195	        await git(io, seats[seat], ['merge', '--ff-only', 'main']);
  4008	   196	      } catch (err) {
  4009	   197	        const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.stderr || /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message || String(err);
  4010	   198	        return { ok: false, reason: `worktree wt-${seat} could not fast-forward to main — corruption signal, parked: ${String(msg).split('\n')[0]}` };
  4011	   199	      }
  4012	   200	    }
  4013	   201	    return { ok: true, branch: input.branch };
  4014	   202	  } finally {
  4015	   203	    try {
  4016	   204	      if (io.fs.existsSync(lock)) io.fs.unlinkSync(lock);
  4017	   205	    } catch {
  4018	   206	      // Best effort — a leaked lock is visible and operator-recoverable.
  4019	   207	    }
  4020	   208	  }
  4021	   209	}
  4022	   210	
  4023	   211	/**
  4024	   212	 * Self-heal a raw-deleted seat: git still lists it but the directory is gone
  4025	   213	 * — prune the stale metadata and re-add on the same branch.
  4026	   214	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string}} target @param {any} io
  4027	   215	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, healed: boolean}>}
  4028	   216	 */
  4029	   217	export async function selfHealWorktree(root, target, io) {
  4030	   218	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
  4031	   219	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
  4032	   220	  const stale = listed.some((e) => e.path === path) && !io.fs.existsSync(path);
  4033	   221	  if (!stale) return { ok: true, healed: false };
  4034	   222	  await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'prune']);
  4035	   223	  await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'add', path, target.branch]);
  4036	   224	  return { ok: true, healed: true };
  4037	   225	}
  4038	   226	
  4039	   227	/**
  4040	   228	 * Tear down BOTH seats: remove the worktrees and delete their namespaced
  4041	   229	 * branches. Anything outside .worktrees/wt-* or baton/wt-* is never touched,
  4042	   230	 * so a teardown leaves the repo a plain git repo (acceptance constraint 5).
  4043	   231	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
  4044	   232	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, removed: string[]}>}
  4045	   233	 */
  4046	   234	export async function teardownWorktrees(root, io) {
  4047	   235	  const p = worktreePaths(root);
  4048	   236	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
  4049	   237	  /** @type {string[]} */
  4050	   238	  const removed = [];
  4051	   239	  for (const seat of SEATS) {
  4052	   240	    const entry = listed.find((e) => e.path === p.seats[seat]);
  4053	   241	    if (!entry) continue;
  4054	   242	    await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'remove', '--force', p.seats[seat]]);
  4055	   243	    // Delete the seat's CURRENT branch and its setup base branch — a seat
  4056	   244	    // that moved on to subtask branches orphans its base otherwise
  4057	   245	    // (acceptance constraint 5: teardown leaves a plain git repo). Only
  4058	   246	    // namespaced branches are ever deleted.
  4059	   247	    /** @type {Set<string>} */
  4060	   248	    const toDelete = new Set();
  4061	   249	    if (entry.branch && WORKTREE_BRANCH_RE.test(entry.branch)) toDelete.add(entry.branch);
  4062	   250	    toDelete.add(seatBranch(seat));
  4063	   251	    for (const branch of toDelete) {
  4064	   252	      if (!WORKTREE_BRANCH_RE.test(branch)) continue;
  4065	   253	      try {
  4066	   254	        await git(io, root, ['branch', '-D', branch]);
  4067	   255	      } catch {
  4068	   256	        // Already gone (current === base, or deleted earlier) — fine.
  4069	   257	      }
  4070	   258	    }
  4071	   259	    removed.push(seat);
  4072	   260	  }
  4073	   261	  return { ok: true, removed };
  4074	   262	}
  4075	   263	
  4076	   264	/**
  4077	   265	 * Branch jail: only baton/wt-* branches may ever be deleted through this
  4078	   266	 * layer. An out-of-namespace ref refuses without touching git.
  4079	   267	 * @param {string} root @param {string} branch @param {any} io
  4080	   268	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, error: string}>}
  4081	   269	 */
  4082	   270	export async function deleteBranch(root, branch, io) {
  4083	   271	  if (!WORKTREE_BRANCH_RE.test(branch)) {
  4084	   272	    return { ok: false, error: `refused: '${branch}' is outside the baton/wt- namespace — the loop never deletes non-loop branches` };
  4085	   273	  }
  4086	   274	  await git(io, root, ['branch', '-D', branch]);
  4087	   275	  return { ok: true };
  4088	   276	}

  4095	     1	// Fake `io` object matching the injected-io contract from docs/design/core.md:
  4096	     2	// { cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now }. Backed by memfs.
  4097	     3	// stdout/stderr collect writes; io.stdoutText()/io.stderrText() flatten them.
  4098	     4	// now() returns a settable ISO string (io.setNow). execFile resolves from an
  4099	     5	// execResults map keyed by the joined command string.
  4100	     6	//
  4101	     7	// Wave-B2 additions (bundle lock layer) — all optional, defaulted, and purely
  4102	     8	// additive so no prior consumer is affected:
  4103	     9	//   host / pid / startTime  — the process-identity facts the lock model writes
  4104	    10	//     into owner.json and compares on contention (see plan §Concurrency, Lock
  4105	    11	//     model iterations 3+4).
  4106	    12	//   processAlive(pid, startTime) — the "provably dead" oracle. Default: only
  4107	    13	//     THIS io's own {pid, startTime} is alive; every other descriptor reads as
  4108	    14	//     dead. Override per test to model a live foreign owner or a pid/start-time
  4109	    15	//     mismatch (reused pid).
  4110	    16	//   newFencingToken() — the injected per-acquisition token source. Deterministic
  4111	    17	//     monotonic counter ("tok-1", "tok-2", …), matching the injected-io
  4112	    18	//     discipline already used for now()/execFile so lock acquisition is
  4113	    19	//     reproducible in tests rather than drawing from Math.random.
  4114	    20	
  4115	    21	import { makeMemfs } from './memfs.mjs';
  4116	    22	
  4117	    23	export function makeIo({
  4118	    24	  files = {},
  4119	    25	  env = {},
  4120	    26	  stdin = '',
  4121	    27	  execResults = {},
  4122	    28	  now = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z',
  4123	    29	  host = 'host-A',
  4124	    30	  pid = 4242,
  4125	    31	  startTime = 111000,
  4126	    32	  processAlive = null,
  4127	    33	  fencingSeed = 1,
  4128	    34	  execPath = process.execPath,
  4129	    35	} = {}) {
  4130	    36	  const { fs, files: filesSnapshot } = makeMemfs(files);
  4131	    37	  const outChunks = [];
  4132	    38	  const errChunks = [];
  4133	    39	  let currentNow = now;
  4134	    40	  let tokenCounter = fencingSeed - 1;
  4135	    41	  const defaultAlive = (p, s) => p === pid && s === startTime;
  4136	    42	
  4137	    43	  const stdout = { write: (s) => { outChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
  4138	    44	  const stderr = { write: (s) => { errChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
  4139	    45	
  4140	    46	  const execFile = (cmd, args = [], /* opts */ _opts = {}) => {
  4141	    47	    const key = [cmd, ...(Array.isArray(args) ? args : [])].join(' ');
  4142	    48	    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  4143	    49	      if (!(key in execResults)) {
  4144	    50	        const e = new Error(`spawn ${cmd} ENOENT`);
  4145	    51	        e.code = 'ENOENT';
  4146	    52	        return reject(e);
  4147	    53	      }
  4148	    54	      const r = execResults[key];
  4149	    55	      const code = r.code ?? 0;
  4150	    56	      const out = r.stdout ?? '';
  4151	    57	      const err = r.stderr ?? '';
  4152	    58	      if (code !== 0) {
  4153	    59	        // util.promisify(execFile) rejects on nonzero exit; error carries code + streams.
  4154	    60	        const e = new Error(`Command failed: ${key}`);
  4155	    61	        e.code = code;
  4156	    62	        e.stdout = out;
  4157	    63	        e.stderr = err;
  4158	    64	        return reject(e);
  4159	    65	      }
  4160	    66	      resolve({ stdout: out, stderr: err });
  4161	    67	    });
  4162	    68	  };
  4163	    69	
  4164	    70	  return {
  4165	    71	    cwd: '/repo',
  4166	    72	    env,
  4167	    73	    stdin,
  4168	    74	    stdout,
  4169	    75	    stderr,
  4170	    76	    fs,
  4171	    77	    execFile,
  4172	    78	    now: () => currentNow,
  4173	    79	    setNow: (iso) => { currentNow = iso; },
  4174	    80	    execPath,
  4175	    81	    host,
  4176	    82	    pid,
  4177	    83	    startTime,
  4178	    84	    processAlive: processAlive || defaultAlive,
  4179	    85	    // Tiny retry budget: unit tests exercising live-lock refusal stay fast
  4180	    86	    // while the retry code path itself still runs (production default: 120x25ms).
  4181	    87	    lockRetry: { attempts: 2, delayMs: 2 },
  4182	    88	    newFencingToken: () => `tok-${++tokenCounter}`,
  4183	    89	    stdoutText: () => outChunks.join(''),
  4184	    90	    stderrText: () => errChunks.join(''),
  4185	    91	    files: filesSnapshot,
  4186	    92	  };
  4187	    93	}
